Conclusion: 该框架通过形式化方法弥合了传统风险工程与现代机器学习之间的鸿沟，为机器人物理安全提供了系统化解决方案

Abstract: This report presents a structured Robotics Physical Safety Framework based on explicit asset declaration, systematic vulnerability enumeration, and hazard-driven synthetic data generation. The approach bridges classical risk engineering with modern machine learning pipelines, enabling safety envelope learning grounded in a formalized hazard ontology. The key contribution of this framework is the alignment between classical safety engineering, digital twin simulation, synthetic data generation, and machine learning model training.

</details>


### [39] [Dual-Agent Multiple-Model Reinforcement Learning for Event-Triggered Human-Robot Co-Adaptation in Decoupled Task Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06163)
*Yaqi Li,Zhengqi Han,Huifang Liu,Steven W. Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于事件触发的双智能体多模型强化学习共享控制康复策略，用于6自由度上肢机器人，通过解耦空间轴和自适应参数调整来优化人机协同


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定频率控制在复杂到达任务中常因逆运动学执行时间变化导致轨迹振荡，需要一种能安全高效优化人机协同适应的方法

Method: 采用事件驱动策略：当末端执行器进入以目标路径点为中心的准入球时触发控制动作；提出DAMMRL框架：人类智能体选择准入球半径（速度-精度权衡），机器人智能体动态调整3D笛卡尔步长（补充用户认知状态）

Result: 在混合环境中训练和部署，该方法有效抑制了路径点抖动，平衡了空间精度与时间效率，显著提高了物体获取任务的成功率

Conclusion: 事件触发的DAMMRL方法为康复机器人提供了一种有效的共享控制策略，通过解耦空间轴和自适应参数调整实现了安全高效的人机协同

Abstract: This paper presents a shared-control rehabilitation policy for a custom 6-degree-of-freedom (6-DoF) upper-limb robot that decomposes complex reaching tasks into decoupled spatial axes. The patient governs the primary reaching direction using binary commands, while the robot autonomously manages orthogonal corrective motions. Because traditional fixed-frequency control often induces trajectory oscillations due to variable inverse-kinematics execution times, an event-driven progression strategy is proposed. This architecture triggers subsequent control actions only when the end-effector enters an admission sphere centred on the immediate target waypoint, and was validated in a semi-virtual setup linking a physical pressure sensor to a MuJoCo simulation. To optimise human--robot co-adaptation safely and efficiently, this study introduces Dual Agent Multiple Model Reinforcement Learning (DAMMRL). This framework discretises decision characteristics: the human agent selects the admission sphere radius to reflect their inherent speed--accuracy trade-off, while the robot agent dynamically adjusts its 3D Cartesian step magnitudes to complement the user's cognitive state. Trained in simulation and deployed across mixed environments, this event-triggered DAMMRL approach effectively suppresses waypoint chatter, balances spatial precision with temporal efficiency, and significantly improves success rates in object acquisition tasks.

</details>


### [40] [DreamToNav: Generalizable Navigation for Robots via Generative Video Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06190)
*Valerii Serpiva,Jeffrin Sam,Chidera Simon,Hajira Amjad,Iana Zhura,Artem Lykov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamToNav：基于生成视频模型的机器人自主导航框架，通过自然语言指令生成视觉描述，合成物理一致的视频序列，从中提取可执行运动轨迹，实现直观的人机交互控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人导航依赖刚性路径点规划，需要专业工程知识。本文旨在通过自然语言交互和生成视频模型，让用户能够直观地控制机器人执行复杂任务，无需特定任务工程。

Method: 1. 使用Qwen 2.5-VL-7B-Instruct将模糊的自然语言指令细化为精确的视觉描述；2. 用NVIDIA Cosmos 2.5视频基础模型生成物理一致的机器人执行任务视频序列；3. 通过视觉姿态估计、机器人检测和轨迹恢复从合成视频中提取有效运动学路径。

Result: 在轮式移动机器人和四足机器人室内导航任务中，DreamToNav达到76.7%的成功率，最终目标误差在0.05-0.10米内，轨迹跟踪误差低于0.15米。结果表明从生成视频预测中提取的轨迹可在不同运动平台上可靠执行。

Conclusion: DreamToNav将视频生成作为规划引擎，让机器人能够"视觉化梦想"复杂行为后再执行，为避障和目标导向导航提供了统一框架，无需任务特定工程，实现了从自然语言到机器人动作的直观转换。

Abstract: We present DreamToNav, a novel autonomous robot framework that uses generative video models to enable intuitive, human-in-the-loop control. Instead of relying on rigid waypoint navigation, users provide natural language prompts (e.g. ``Follow the person carefully''), which the system translates into executable motion. Our pipeline first employs Qwen 2.5-VL-7B-Instruct to refine vague user instructions into precise visual descriptions. These descriptions condition NVIDIA Cosmos 2.5, a state-of-the-art video foundation model, to synthesize a physically consistent video sequence of the robot performing the task. From this synthetic video, we extract a valid kinematic path using visual pose estimation, robot detection and trajectory recovery. By treating video generation as a planning engine, DreamToNav allows robots to visually "dream" complex behaviors before executing them, providing a unified framework for obstacle avoidance and goal-directed navigation without task-specific engineering. We evaluate the approach on both a wheeled mobile robot and a quadruped robot in indoor navigation tasks. DreamToNav achieves a success rate of 76.7%, with final goal errors typically within 0.05-0.10 m and trajectory tracking errors below 0.15 m. These results demonstrate that trajectories extracted from generative video predictions can be reliably executed on physical robots across different locomotion platforms.

</details>


### [41] [Towards Robotic Lake Maintenance: Integrating SONAR and Satellite Data to Assist Human Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06266)
*Ahmed H. Elsayed,Christoph Manss,Tarek A. El-Mihoub,Andrej Lejman,Frederic Stahl*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种两阶段方法，结合卫星遥感和无人水面船声纳技术，用于人工水体中水下植被的检测与精准收割管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工水体需要持续监测和维护以管理其生态系统，其中水下植被会快速生长，需要定期收割以维持生态平衡。传统方法劳动强度大且效率低，需要更智能的监测和管理方案。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：第一阶段使用卫星遥感数据，通过水生植物和藻类指数检测水下植被区域；第二阶段使用配备多波束声纳的无人水面船进行高分辨率水深测绘，精确定位和量化水生植被。

Result: 初步结果表明，卫星图像与水下声学传感的集成在人工湖泊植被管理中具有可行性。该方法实现了人机协作，卫星数据指导无人船任务，声纳地图支持精准收割，缩小了搜索范围并减少了人工工作量。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的两阶段方法为人工水体中水下植被的监测和收割管理提供了有效的技术方案，通过卫星遥感与无人船声纳技术的结合，提高了植被管理的效率和精准度，降低了人工劳动强度。

Abstract: Artificial Water Bodies (AWBs) are human-made systems that require continuous monitoring due to their artificial biological processes. These systems demand regular maintenance to manage their ecosystems effectively. As a result of these artificial conditions, underwater vegetation can grow rapidly and must be harvested to preserve the ecological balance. This paper proposes a two-step approach to support targeted weed harvesting for the maintenance of artificial lakes. The first step is the initial detection of Submerged Aquatic Vegetation (SAV), also referred to in this paper as areas of interest, is performed using satellite-derived indices, specifically the Aquatic Plants and Algae (APA) index, which highlights submerged vegetation in water bodies. Subsequently, an Unmanned Surface Vehicle (USV) equipped with multibeam SOund NAvigation and Ranging (SONAR) performs high-resolution bathymetric mapping to locate and quantify aquatic vegetation precisely. This two-stage approach offers an effective human-robot collaboration, where satellite data guides the USV missions and boat skippers leverage detailed SONAR maps for targeted harvesting. This setup narrows the search space and reduces manual workload from human operators, making the harvesting process less labour-intensive for operators. Preliminary results demonstrate the feasibility of integrating satellite imagery and underwater acoustic sensing to improve vegetation management in artificial lakes.

</details>


### [42] [SuperSuit: An Isomorphic Bimodal Interface for Scalable Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06280)
*Tongqing Chen,Hang Wu,Jiasen Wang,Xiaotao Li,Zhu Jin,Lu Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SuperSuit是一个用于轮式移动机械臂的双模态数据采集框架，支持遥操作和主动演示两种模式，通过共享运动学接口生成结构一致的关节空间轨迹，解决了长时程移动操作数据获取的瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 获取高质量、长时程的演示数据对于具身AI至关重要，但对于轮式移动机械臂来说，现有遥操作和可穿戴接口存在局限性，需要连续协调SE(2)移动和精确操作，数据获取成为基本瓶颈。

Method: 提出SuperSuit双模态数据采集框架：1) 支持机器人遥操作和主动演示两种模式；2) 两种模式共享运动学接口，生成结构一致的关节空间轨迹；3) 通过自然人类步态映射到连续平面基座速度；4) 使用严格同构的可穿戴手臂；5) 采用平移不变的delta关节表示进行策略训练以减轻校准偏移和结构柔度。

Result: 在真实世界长时程移动操作任务中：1) 主动模式演示吞吐量比遥操作基线高2.6倍；2) 在固定数据集大小下，用主动演示替代遥操作数据可获得可比的策略性能；3) 随着主动数据量增加，性能呈单调改善。

Conclusion: 跨采集模态的一致运动学表示能够实现长时程移动操作的可扩展数据获取，SuperSuit框架通过共享接口解决了移动机械臂数据获取的瓶颈问题。

Abstract: High-quality, long-horizon demonstrations are essential for embodied AI, yet acquiring such data for tightly coupled wheeled mobile manipulators remains a fundamental bottleneck. Unlike fixed-base systems, mobile manipulators require continuous coordination between $SE(2)$ locomotion and precise manipulation, exposing limitations in existing teleoperation and wearable interfaces. We present \textbf{SuperSuit}, a bimodal data acquisition framework that supports both robot-in-the-loop teleoperation and active demonstration under a shared kinematic interface. Both modalities produce structurally identical joint-space trajectories, enabling direct data mixing without modifying downstream policies. For locomotion, SuperSuit maps natural human stepping to continuous planar base velocities, eliminating discrete command switches. For manipulation, it employs a strictly isomorphic wearable arm in both modes, while policy training is formulated in a shift-invariant delta-joint representation to mitigate calibration offsets and structural compliance without inverse kinematics. Real-world experiments on long-horizon mobile manipulation tasks show 2.6$\times$ higher demonstration throughput in active mode compared to a teleoperation baseline, comparable policy performance when substituting teleoperation data with active demonstrations at fixed dataset size, and monotonic performance improvement as active data volume increases. These results indicate that consistent kinematic representations across collection modalities enable scalable data acquisition for long-horizon mobile manipulation.

</details>


### [43] [Safe Consensus of Cooperative Manipulation with Hierarchical Event-Triggered Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06356)
*Simiao Zhuang,Bingkun Huang,Zewen Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 分布式控制框架通过分层事件触发控制屏障函数实现多机械臂协同运输的安全共识协调


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机械臂协同运输重型或大型负载需要在变化环境中实现协调的编队跟踪，同时满足严格的安全约束，且面临有限通信和实时计算预算的挑战

Method: 提出基于分层事件触发控制屏障函数的分布式控制框架：1) 基于局部邻居信息的共识协议实现任务空间平移和旋转一致性；2) 三层分层事件触发安全架构；3) 风险感知领导者选择和平滑切换策略降低在线计算

Result: 通过两台Franka机械臂硬件实验和综合仿真验证：在静态和动态障碍物环境下实现更高精度的协同操作，相比基线方法显著降低计算成本和通信频率

Conclusion: 该框架为多机械臂协同运输提供了具有安全保证的分布式控制解决方案，在有限通信和计算资源下实现了高效、安全的协同操作

Abstract: Cooperative transport and manipulation of heavy or bulky payloads by multiple manipulators requires coordinated formation tracking, while simultaneously enforcing strict safety constraints in varying environments with limited communication and real-time computation budgets. This paper presents a distributed control framework that achieves consensus coordination with safety guarantees via hierarchical event-triggered control barrier functions (CBFs). We first develop a consensus-based protocol that relies solely on local neighbor information to enforce both translational and rotational consistency in task space. Building on this coordination layer, we propose a three-level hierarchical event-triggered safety architecture with CBFs, which is integrated with a risk-aware leader selection and smooth switching strategy to reduce online computation. The proposed approach is validated through real-world hardware experiments using two Franka manipulators operating with static obstacles, as well as comprehensive simulations demonstrating scalable multi-arm cooperation with dynamic obstacles. Results demonstrate higher precision cooperation under strict safety constraints, achieving substantially reduced computational cost and communication frequency compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [44] [Data Analogies Enable Efficient Cross-Embodiment Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06450)
*Jonathan Yang,Chelsea Finn,Dorsa Sadigh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了如何组织异构机器人演示数据以实现跨机器人设置的迁移，发现不同形式的多样性对不同类型的迁移（感知vs形态）效果不同，数据类比比简单扩大规模更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略在多种机器人、场景和视角的演示数据上训练，但如何有效组织和扩展这种异构数据以真正提升特定目标设置下的性能仍不清楚。研究旨在确定哪种形式的演示数据最能促进跨机器人设置的迁移。

Method: 通过控制实验，系统地改变末端执行器形态、机器人平台外观和相机视角，比较单纯增加演示数量与以不同方式系统扩展多样性的效果。在模拟环境中进行实验，分析不同数据组织方式对跨机器人迁移的影响。

Result: 模拟实验显示：感知偏移（如视角变化）最受益于广泛的多样性，而形态偏移从非结构化多样性中获益较少，最大增益来自数据类比（即在不同机器人之间对齐场景、任务和轨迹的配对演示）。基于模拟结果，仅通过改变数据组成，在真实世界中将跨机器人迁移成功率平均提升了22.5%。

Conclusion: 跨机器人迁移的有效性取决于迁移类型：感知迁移需要广泛的多样性，而形态迁移则更需要结构化的数据类比。仅扩大数据规模不如精心设计数据组成有效，通过数据类比可以显著提升真实世界的跨机器人迁移性能。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies are trained on demonstrations collected across a wide variety of robots, scenes, and viewpoints. Yet it remains unclear how to best organize and scale such heterogeneous data so that it genuinely improves performance in a given target setting. In this work, we ask: what form of demonstration data is most useful for enabling transfer across robot set-ups? We conduct controlled experiments that vary end-effector morphology, robot platform appearance, and camera perspective, and compare the effects of simply scaling the number of demonstrations against systematically broadening the diversity in different ways. Our simulated experiments show that while perceptual shifts such as viewpoint benefit most from broad diversity, morphology shifts benefit far less from unstructured diversity and instead see the largest gains from data analogies, i.e. paired demonstrations that align scenes, tasks, and/or trajectories across different embodiments. Informed by the simulation results, we improve real-world cross-embodiment transfer success by an average of $22.5\%$ over large-scale, unpaired datasets by changing only the composition of the data.

</details>


### [45] [History-Conditioned Spatio-Temporal Visual Token Pruning for Efficient Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06480)
*Qitong Wang,Yijun Liang,Ming Li,Tianyi Zhou,Christopher Rasmussen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的时空视觉token剪枝框架，用于VLA模型的视觉语言导航，在保持导航精度的同时显著降低计算延迟，实现实时机器人部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言-动作模型在导航任务中表现出色，但计算成本高导致延迟问题，限制了在实时机器人系统中的实际部署。需要一种既能保持导航性能又能提高推理效率的解决方案。

Method: 提出无需训练的时空视觉token剪枝框架：1) 对当前视图应用空间token选择；2) 对历史记忆进行时空压缩；3) 基于注意力的token重要性评估和查询引导的时空过滤；4) 无需重新训练或修改预训练模型，实现即插即用集成。

Result: 在标准VLN基准测试中显著优于现有剪枝策略，在极端剪枝场景下仍能保持优越的导航精度，同时保持高度竞争力的推理效率。在Unitree Go2四足机器人上的实际部署验证了在现实机器人约束下的可靠低延迟指令跟随导航。

Conclusion: 该工作有助于弥合大规模多模态建模与高效实时具身部署之间的差距，为机器人导航系统提供了实用的解决方案，实现了计算效率与导航性能的良好平衡。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) enables robots to follow natural-language instructions in visually grounded environments, serving as a key capability for embodied robotic systems. Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong navigation performance, but their high computational cost introduces latency that limits real-time deployment. We propose a training-free spatio-temporal vision token pruning framework tailored to VLA-based VLN. We apply spatial token selection to the current view, alongside spatio-temporal compression for historical memories, enabling efficient long-horizon inference while reducing redundant computation. Leveraging attention-based token importance and query-guided spatio-temporal filtering, the proposed approach preserves navigation-relevant information without retraining or modifying pretrained models, allowing plug-and-play integration into existing VLA systems. Through experiments on standard VLN benchmarks, we confirm that our method significantly outperforms existing pruning strategies. It successfully preserves superior navigation accuracy under extreme pruning scenarios, all while maintaining the highly competitive inference efficiency. Real-world deployment on a Unitree Go2 quadruped robot further validates reliable and low-latency instruction-following navigation under practical robotic constraints. We hope this work helps bridge the gap between large-scale multimodal modeling and efficient, real-time embodied deployment in robotic navigation systems.

</details>


### [46] [CFEAR-Teach-and-Repeat: Fast and Accurate Radar-only Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06501)
*Maximilian Hilger,Daniel Adolfsson,Ralf Becker,Henrik Andreasson,Achim J. Lilienthal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CFEAR-TR：一种基于单旋转雷达的"教导-重复"定位方法，用于恶劣天气条件下的鲁棒自主导航，通过联合对齐实时扫描与存储地图及滑动窗口关键帧实现高精度定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在恶劣天气条件下，光学传感器可能失效，因此需要可靠的地图先验定位方法。雷达传感器在恶劣天气中表现稳定，但现有雷达定位方法精度不足，特别是在航向估计方面与激光雷达存在差距。

Method: 使用单旋转雷达构建"教导-重复"定位管道。采用稀疏定向表面点表示雷达扫描（基于多普勒补偿测量）。定位时联合对齐实时扫描与存储的地图扫描以及最近实时关键帧的滑动窗口。地图以位姿图形式存储，定位时遍历该图。

Result: 在Boreas数据集的测试序列上，CFEAR-TR定位精度达到0.117米和0.096°，相比先前最优方法提升高达63%。运行效率为29Hz，显著缩小了与激光雷达定位的差距，特别是在航向估计方面。

Conclusion: CFEAR-TR实现了恶劣天气条件下基于雷达的高精度、轻量级、鲁棒定位，接近激光雷达性能水平，为自主导航提供了可靠解决方案。C++实现已开源。

Abstract: Reliable localization in prior maps is essential for autonomous navigation, particularly under adverse weather, where optical sensors may fail. We present CFEAR-TR, a teach-and-repeat localization pipeline using a single spinning radar, which is designed for easily deployable, lightweight, and robust navigation in adverse conditions. Our method localizes by jointly aligning live scans to both stored scans from the teach mapping pass, and to a sliding window of recent live keyframes. This ensures accurate and robust pose estimation across different seasons and weather phenomena. Radar scans are represented using a sparse set of oriented surface points, computed from Doppler-compensated measurements. The map is stored in a pose graph that is traversed during localization. Experiments on the held-out test sequences from the Boreas dataset show that CFEAR-TR can localize with an accuracy as low as 0.117 m and 0.096°, corresponding to improvements of up to 63% over the previous state of the art, while running efficiently at 29 Hz. These results substantially narrow the gap to lidar-level localization, particularly in heading estimation. We make the C++ implementation of our work available to the community.

</details>

*Neil R. Wagner,Justin K. Yim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种仅使用三个执行器的欠驱动单足机器人，通过滚动、跳跃和空中重定向实现低重力环境下的多模态运动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计用于低重力天体探索的多模态运动机器人，通过简单控制系统实现滚动、跳跃和着陆等多种运动模式，以适应不同的地形和环境条件

Method: 使用仅两个反作用轮控制空间姿态，配备平衡控制器（地面跳跃方向控制）和空中重定向控制器（着陆腿姿态控制），通过三个执行器实现模式切换

Result: 成功演示了滚动、目标跳跃和着陆、以及自恢复功能，系统尺寸为0.33米，重量1.25公斤，仅使用三个执行器

Conclusion: 通过简单的多模态运动切换，该系统能够有效应对不同的地形和环境条件，为低重力天体探索提供了一种轻量、高效的机器人解决方案

Abstract: We present a rolling and jumping underactuated monopedal robot designed to explore multimodal locomotion on low-gravity bodies. It uses only two reaction wheels to control its spatial orientation with two controllers: a balancing controller which can aim the robot's jump direction on the ground, and an aerial reorientation controller which can aim the robot's leg for landing after flight. We demonstrate rolling, targeted jumping and landing, and self-righting using only three actuators total, keeping system size to 0.33m and 1.25kg. Simple switching between locomotion modes enables the system to deal with differing landscapes and environmental conditions.

</details>


### [48] [Unified Learning of Temporal Task Structure and Action Timing for Bimanual Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06538)
*Christian Dreher,Patrick Dormanns,Andre Meixner,Tamim Asfour*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种从人类演示中学习符号和亚符号时间任务约束的方法，用于生成可执行的双手机器人操作计划


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将高层任务规划和低层运动同步分开处理，导致符号时间关系（用于高层推理）和具体时间参数（用于执行协调）之间存在鸿沟，需要统一框架来学习这两类时间约束

Method: 1) 使用三维时间表示和多元高斯混合模型学习动作间的亚符号时间关系；2) 基于DPLL算法寻找所有无矛盾的Allen关系分配，表示任务的不同模式；3) 结合符号和亚符号约束，通过优化规划系统生成时间参数化计划

Result: 在多个数据集上评估，该方法生成的时间参数化计划比最具代表性的演示基线更接近人类演示

Conclusion: 提出的方法成功填补了高层任务规划和低层运动同步之间的鸿沟，能够从演示中学习完整的时间任务约束并生成可执行的双手机器人操作计划

Abstract: Temporal task structure is fundamental for bimanual manipulation: a robot must not only know that one action precedes or overlaps another, but also when each action should occur and how long it should take. While symbolic temporal relations enable high-level reasoning about task structure and alternative execution sequences, concrete timing parameters are equally essential for coordinating two hands at the execution level. Existing approaches address these two levels in isolation, leaving a gap between high-level task planning and low-level movement synchronization. This work presents an approach for learning both symbolic and subsymbolic temporal task constraints from human demonstrations and deriving executable, temporally parametrized plans for bimanual manipulation. Our contributions are (i) a 3-dimensional representation of timings between two actions with methods based on multivariate Gaussian Mixture Models to represent temporal relationships between actions on a subsymbolic level, (ii) a method based on the Davis-Putnam-Logemann-Loveland (DPLL) algorithm that finds and ranks all contradiction-free assignments of Allen relations to action pairs, representing different modes of a task, and (iii) an optimization-based planning system that combines the identified symbolic and subsymbolic temporal task constraints to derive temporally parametrized plans for robot execution. We evaluate our approach on several datasets, demonstrating that our method generates temporally parametrized plans closer to human demonstrations than the most characteristic demonstration baseline.

</details>

### [49] [Model Change for Description Logic Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05562)
*Ana Ozaki,Jandson S. Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了描述逻辑概念在模型变化下的修改问题，提出了驱逐、接纳和修订三种变化类型，并探讨了它们在EL和ALC描述逻辑中的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究描述逻辑概念在面临以指向解释形式表示的模型时的修改问题。需要形式化处理概念在模型变化下的调整机制，区分不同类型的模型变化操作。

Method: 引入模型变化的形式化框架，定义三种主要变化类型：驱逐（仅移除模型）、接纳（仅纳入模型）和修订（结合移除和纳入）。分析这些操作在EL和ALC描述逻辑中的兼容性关系。

Result: 提供了关于EL和ALC描述逻辑概念中驱逐与接纳兼容性的正反结果，以及关于ALC概念修订兼容性的结果。特别指出修订不能简单归结为驱逐和接纳的组合，这与直觉相反。

Conclusion: 模型变化是描述逻辑概念修改的重要问题，修订操作具有独特的复杂性，不能简化为驱逐和接纳的简单组合。在EL和ALC等描述逻辑中，不同变化类型的兼容性存在理论上的限制和可能性。

Abstract: We consider the problem of modifying a description logic concept in light of models represented as pointed interpretations. We call this setting model change, and distinguish three main kinds of changes: eviction, which consists of only removing models; reception, which incorporates models; and revision, which combines removal with incorporation of models in a single operation. We introduce a formal notion of revision and argue that it does not reduce to a simple combination of eviction and reception, contrary to intuition. We provide positive and negative results on the compatibility of eviction and reception for EL and ALC description logic concepts and on the compatibility of revision for ALC concepts.

</details>


### [50] [LTLGuard: Formalizing LTL Specifications with Compact Language Models and Lightweight Symbolic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05728)
*Medina Andresel,Cristinel Mateis,Dejan Nickovic,Spyridon Kounoupidis,Panagiotis Katsaros,Stavros Tripakis*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: LTLGuard：一个模块化工具链，通过约束生成和形式一致性检查，使资源高效的开源模型（4B-14B参数）能够从非正式需求生成正确的线性时序逻辑（LTL）规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将非正式需求转化为形式化规范具有挑战性，因为自然语言存在歧义性和变异性。这一挑战在使用紧凑（中小型）语言模型时尤为突出，这些模型可能缺乏对时序逻辑的稳健知识，难以生成语法有效且一致的形式化规范。

Method: 提出LTLGuard模块化工具链，结合约束生成与形式一致性检查，从非正式输入生成无冲突的LTL规范。该方法将语言模型的生成能力与轻量级自动推理工具集成，迭代细化候选规范，理解冲突根源并消除不一致性。

Result: 展示了该方法的可用性和有效性，并对最终框架进行了定量评估，证明资源高效的开源模型能够生成正确的LTL规范。

Conclusion: LTLGuard通过结合语言模型生成和形式化验证，解决了紧凑模型在生成正确LTL规范方面的局限性，为从非正式需求到形式化规范的转换提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Translating informal requirements into formal specifications is challenging due to the ambiguity and variability of natural language (NL). This challenge is particularly pronounced when relying on compact (small and medium) language models, which may lack robust knowledge of temporal logic and thus struggle to produce syntactically valid and consistent formal specifications. In this work, we focus on enabling resource-efficient open-weight models (4B--14B parameters) to generate correct linear temporal logic (LTL) specifications from informal requirements. We present LTLGuard, a modular toolchain that combines constrained generation with formal consistency checking to generate conflict-free LTL specifications from informal input. Our method integrates the generative capabilities of model languages with lightweight automated reasoning tools to iteratively refine candidate specifications, understand the origin of the conflicts and thus help in eliminating inconsistencies. We demonstrate the usability and the effectiveness of our approach and perform quantitative evaluation of the resulting framework.

</details>


### [51] [Diagonalizing Through the $ω$-Chain: Iterated Self-Certification on Bounded Turing Machines and its Least Fixed Point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06012)
*Miara Sung*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文将图灵机自认证的时间开销问题转化为域论框架，通过定义时间边界推进算子，证明有界计算无法达到不动点，但迭代过程形成ω链，其Scott极限给出最小不动点，对应无界计算完全捕获停机行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图灵机有界自认证失败的原因是自模拟必然产生严格正的时间开销。论文旨在将这一操作约束转化为域论框架，为停机问题提供新的视角。

Method: 定义了一个算子，将有限停机观察从时间边界i推进到i+1。证明有界机器无法在该算子下达到不动点，但迭代过程形成上升ω链。通过计算该链的Scott极限得到算子的最小不动点。

Result: 有界计算无法达到固定点，但迭代过程形成ω链，其Scott极限解析为算子的最小不动点，代表一个无界计算，完全捕获机器的停机行为。

Conclusion: 该构造为停机问题提供了新的视角，将从有限可观测性到最小不动点的过渡框架化为对角线的连续推迟，揭示了有界与无界计算之间的本质联系。

Abstract: Bounded self-certification in Turing machines fails because self-simulation necessarily incurs a strictly positive temporal overhead. We translate this operational constraint into a domain-theoretic framework, defining an operator that advances a finite halting observation from time bound $i$ to $i+1$. While no bounded machine can achieve a fixed point under this operator, the iterative process forms an ascending $ω$-chain. The Scott limit of this chain resolves to the least fixed point of the operator, representing an unbounded computation that fully captures the machine's halting behavior. Our construction provides a novel perspective on the halting problem, framing the transition from finite observability to the least fixed point as the continuous deferral of the diagonal.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [52] [Hybrid Structured Editing: Structures for Tools, Text for Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05644)
*Tom Beckmann,Christoph Thiede,Jens Lincke,Robert Hirschfeld*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出混合结构化编辑方法，在保持文本编辑界面的同时提供结构保证，支持编程工具与源代码的紧密集成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编程环境扩展机制难以实现工具与源代码的紧密集成，工具需要跟踪程序结构变化但文本解析步骤使结构跟踪复杂化

Method: 提出混合结构化编辑方法，允许工具构建者声明程序结构约束并确保遵守，在提供结构保证的同时保持熟悉的文本编辑界面

Result: 实现混合结构化编辑系统并通过多个案例研究展示其有效性，支持在结构化代码表示上工作的工具扩展

Conclusion: 混合结构化编辑支持编程环境的安全扩展，使工具能够在结构化代码表示上工作并提供一致可靠的用户体验

Abstract: In programming, better tools often yield better results. For that, modern programming environments offer mechanisms to allow for their extensibility. The closer those tools are to the code, the easier it is for programmers to map the information provided by a tool to the code this information is about.  However, existing extension mechanisms do not facilitate the close integration of tools with textual source code. Tools must be able to track program structures across edits to appear at the right positions but the parsing step of text complicates tracking structures. We propose hybrid structured editing, an approach that supports tool builders by providing structural guarantees while providing tool users with a familiar and consistent text editing interface. Hybrid structured editing allows tool builders to declare constraints on the structure that a program must conform to and ensures their observance. We present an implementation and several case studies of tools based on hybrid structured editing to demonstrate its effectiveness. Hybrid structured editing supports the safe extension of programming environments with tools that work on a structured representation of code and provide a consistent and reliable user experience.

</details>


### [53] [Pitfalls in VM Implementation on CHERI: Lessons from Porting CRuby](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05645)
*Hanhaotian Liu,Tetsuro Yamazaki,Tomoharu Ugawa*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文通过将CRuby移植到CHERI架构的案例研究，系统分析了虚拟机在CHERI平台上开发时遇到的特定陷阱，特别是由C语言未定义行为与传统架构假设冲突引起的问题，并提供了相应的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CHERI硬件通过能力指针增强内存安全性，虚拟机作为可能包含内存漏洞的系统能够从中受益。然而，将虚拟机移植到CHERI面临挑战，因为传统架构下基于C语言未定义行为的假设与CHERI更严格的内存安全模型冲突，导致意外故障。现有移植指南主要关注通用编程，缺乏针对虚拟机特定问题的指导。

Method: 通过将CRuby移植到CHERI作为案例研究，并调研先前将虚拟机移植到CHERI的工作。基于问题原因对发现的问题进行分类和讨论，针对每个类别说明虚拟机特定的陷阱。特别关注C语言未定义行为，以及虚拟机实现技术和惯用法对传统架构行为的假设在CHERI上无效的情况。讨论相应的变通方案及其影响，并通过在CRuby移植中应用这些方案和调研先前案例研究的代码库来验证变通方案的有效性。

Result: 识别并分类了虚拟机在CHERI平台上的特定陷阱，发现大多数陷阱源于C语言的未定义行为。具体而言，虚拟机的实现技术和惯用法通常假设传统架构的行为，这些假设在CHERI上无效。提供了针对这些问题的变通方案，并验证了这些方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 这项工作为在CHERI上开发和移植虚拟机提供了知识积累，有助于指导构建更安全的虚拟机。通过系统分析虚拟机特定的陷阱和提供解决方案，填补了现有指南在虚拟机特定问题方面的空白。

Abstract: CHERI (Capability Hardware Enhanced RISC Instructions) is a novel hardware designed to address memory safety issues. By replacing traditional pointers with hardware capabilities, it enhances security in modern software systems. A Virtual Machine (VM) is one such system that can benefit from CHERI's protection, as it may contain latent memory vulnerabilities. However, developing and porting VMs to CHERI is a non-trivial task. There are many subtle pitfalls from the assumptions on the undefined behaviors of the C language made based on conventional architectures. Those assumptions conflict with CHERI's stricter memory safety model, causing unexpected failures. Although several prior works have discussed the process of porting VMs, they focus on the overall porting process instead of the pitfalls for VM implementation on CHERI. The guide for programming in CHERI exists, but it is for general programming, not addressing VM-specific issues. We have ported CRuby to CHERI as a case study and surveyed previous works on porting VMs to CHERI. We categorized and discussed the issues found based on their causes. In this paper, we illustrate the VM-specific pitfalls for each category. Most of the pitfalls arise from the undefined behaviors in the C language; in particular, implementation techniques and idioms of VMs often assume behaviors of traditional architectures that are invalid on CHERI. We also discuss workarounds for them and the impacts of those workarounds. We verified the validity of the workarounds by applying them to our CRuby port and by surveying the codebases of prior case studies. This work contributes to the body of knowledge on developing and porting VMs to CHERI and will help guide efforts toward constructing safer VMs.

</details>


### [54] [Evaluating LLMs in the Context of a Functional Programming Course: A Comprehensive Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05646)
*Yihan Zhang,Brigitte Pientka,Xujie Si*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 评估大语言模型在低资源编程语言OCaml教育环境中的有效性，构建三个基准测试评估9个先进LLM，发现它们在功能编程课程中有效但解决作业问题的能力低于Python/Java环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注高资源编程语言（如Java、Python）中LLM的教育应用效果，但对低资源编程语言（如OCaml）在功能编程教育环境中的有效性缺乏系统评估，需要填补这一研究空白。

Method: 构建三个综合基准测试：1) λCodeGen（自然语言作业编程问题）；2) λRepair（包含语法、类型和逻辑错误的学生提交程序）；3) λExplain（编程理论概念的自然语言问题）。使用OCaml编译器和自动评分器评估9个先进LLM的正确性，并采用人工评分评估回答质量。

Result: 前三个LLM在典型功能编程课程的所有任务中都表现有效，但在低资源环境下解决的作业问题数量远少于在Python和Java入门编程问题中的成功案例。LLM的优势在于纠正语法和类型错误以及回答基本概念问题。

Conclusion: 虽然LLM在某些领域可能还无法与专用语言特定工具匹敌，但其作为多编程语言一站式工具的便利性可以超越更专业系统的优势。该基准可用于评估LLM能力演变、帮助学生认识LLM生成解决方案的局限性，并为编程语言研究者提供开发低资源语言代码合成和修复工具的机会。

Abstract: Large-Language Models (LLMs) are changing the way learners acquire knowledge outside the classroom setting. Previous studies have shown that LLMs seem effective in generating to short and simple questions in introductory CS courses using high-resource programming languages such as Java or Python. In this paper, we evaluate the effectiveness of LLMs in the context of a low-resource programming language -- OCaml, in an educational setting. In particular, we built three benchmarks to comprehensively evaluate 9 state-of-the-art LLMs: 1) $λ$CodeGen (a benchmark containing natural-language homework programming problems); 2) $λ$Repair (a benchmark containing programs with syntax, type, and logical errors drawn from actual student submissions); 3) $λ$Explain (a benchmark containing natural language questions regarding theoretical programming concepts). We grade each LLMs responses with respect to correctness using the OCaml compiler and an autograder. And our evaluation goes beyond common evaluation methodology by using manual grading to assess the quality of the responses. 
  Our study shows that the top three LLMs are effective on all tasks within a typical functional programming course, although they solve much fewer homework problems in the low-resource setting compared to their success on introductory programming problems in Python and Java. The strength of LLMs lies in correcting syntax and type errors as well as generating answers to basic conceptual questions. While LLMs may not yet match dedicated language-specific tools in some areas, their convenience as a one-stop tool for multiple programming languages can outweigh the benefits of more specialized systems. We hope our benchmarks can serve multiple purposes: to assess the evolving capabilities of LLMs, to help instructors raise awareness among students about the limitations of LLM-generated solutions, and to inform programming language researchers about opportunities to integrate domain-specific reasoning into LLMs and develop more powerful code synthesis and repair tools for low-resource languages.

</details>


### [55] [JoinActors: A Modular Library for Actors with Join Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05648)
*Ayman Hussein,Philipp Haller,Ioannis Karras,Hernán Melgratti,Alceste Scalas,Emilio Tuosto*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: JoinActors库在Scala 3中实现了模块化的join模式匹配工具包，通过元编程提供直观API，支持多种匹配算法并显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有join模式实现多为领域特定语言，难以集成到现有生态系统，且匹配算法固定无法优化。需要开发一个既能作为常规库使用，又支持可扩展匹配算法设计的工具包。

Method: 利用Scala 3的元编程技术实现直观的API（类似标准模式匹配），采用模块化设计支持多种匹配算法集成，改进JoinActors库并详细分析其设计实现。

Result: 改进后的JoinActors库保持了与原始库相同的匹配语义，但性能显著提升。通过基准测试验证了不同算法在不同工作负载下的表现，展示了模块化设计的优势。

Conclusion: JoinActors展示了在现有语言中集成高级并发编程构造的可行性，其模块化设计为研究和比较新的join模式匹配算法提供了平台，在表达性、有效性和可用性方面表现出色。

Abstract: Join patterns are a high-level programming construct for message-passing applications. They offer an intuitive and declarative approach for specifying how concurrent and distributed components coordinate, possibly depending on complex conditions over combinations of messages. Join patterns have inspired many implementations -- but most of them are not available as libraries: rather, they are domain-specific languages that can be hard to integrate into pre-existing ecosystems. Moreover, all implementations ship with a predefined matching algorithm, which may not be optimal depending on the application requirements. These limitations are addressed by `JoinActors`, a recently published library which integrates join patterns in the off-the-shelf Scala 3 programming language, and is designed to be modular w.r.t. the matching algorithm in use.
  In this work we address the problem of designing, developing, and evaluating a modular join pattern matching toolkit that (1) can be used as a regular library with a developer-friendly syntax within a pre-existing programming language, and (2) has an extensible design that supports the use and comparison of different matching algorithms. We analyse how `JoinActors` achieves goals (1) and (2) above. The paper that introduced `JoinActors` only briefly outlined its design and implementation (as its main goal was formalising its novel fair matching semantics*). In this work we present and discuss in detail an improved version of `JoinActors`, focusing on its use of metaprogramming (which enables an intuitive API resembling standard pattern matching) and on its modular design. We show how this enables the integration of multiple matching algorithms with different optimisations and we evaluate their performance via benchmarks covering different workloads.
  We illustrate a sophisticated use of Scala 3's metaprogramming for the integration of an advanced concurrent programming construct within a pre-existing language. In addition, we discuss the insights and "lessons learned" in optimising join pattern matching, and how they are facilitated by `JoinActors`'s modularity -- which allows for the systematic comparison of multiple matching algorithm implementations. We adopt the fair join pattern matching semantics and the benchmark suite from the paper that originally introduced `JoinActors`. Through extensive testing we ensure that our new optimised matching algorithms produce exactly the same matches as the original `JoinActors` library, while achieving significantly better performance. The improved version of `JoinActors` is the companion artifact of this paper. This work showcases the expressiveness, effectiveness, and usability of join patterns for implementing complex coordination patterns in distributed message-passing systems, within a pre-existing language. It also demonstrates promising performance results, with significant improvements over previous work. Besides the practical promise, `JoinActors`'s modular design offers a research playground for exploring and comparing new join pattern matching algorithms, possibly based on entirely different semantics.

</details>


### [56] [Efficient Selection of Type Annotations for Performance Improvement in Gradual Typing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05649)
*Senxi Li,Feng Dai,Tetsuro Yamazaki,Shigeru Chiba*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于类型推断的轻量级类型注解选择技术，用于改善渐进类型化程序的执行性能，同时保持较短的编译时间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐进类型化语言中，部分类型化程序可能出现意外的性能下降问题，现有方法虽然能通过选择性添加类型注解来改善性能，但编译时间过长，阻碍了实际应用

Method: 提出一种新技术，从类型推断结果中选择类型注解子集，采用轻量级、摊销的方法，沿着数据流选择类型注解，避免值在无类型和类型化代码边界间反复跨越导致的昂贵运行时类型检查

Result: 在Reticulated Python上的实验表明，该方法优于使用所有类型推断结果的朴素策略；与现有方法相比，获得相当的执行速度，同时在推导和选择类型注解时保持更稳定的编译时间

Conclusion: 所提出的技术在Reticulated Python中是实用的，能够有效缓解渐进类型化程序的性能瓶颈问题

Abstract: Gradual typing has gained popularity as a design choice for integrating static and dynamic typing within a single language. Several practical languages have adopted gradual typing to offer programmers the flexibility to annotate their programs as needed. Meanwhile there is a key challenge of unexpected performance degradation in partially typed programs. The execution speed may significantly decrease when simply adding more type annotations. Prior studies have investigated strategies of selectively adding type annotations for better performance. However, they are restricted in substantial compilation time, which impedes the practical usage.
  This paper presents a new technique to select a subset of type annotations derived by type inference for improving the execution performance of gradually typed programs. The advantage of the proposal is shorter compilation time by employing a lightweight, amortized approach. It selects type annotations along the data flows, which is expected to avoid expensive runtime casts caused by a value repeatedly crossing the boundaries between untyped and typed code.
  We demonstrate the applicability of our proposal, and conduct experiments to validate its effectiveness of improving the execution time on Reticulated Python. Our implementation supports a Python subset to select type annotations derived by an implemented, external type inference engine. Experiment results show that our proposal outperforms a naive strategy of using all type annotations derived by type inference among the benchmark programs. In comparison with an existing approach, the proposal achieves comparable execution speed and shows advantage of maintaining a more stable compilation time of deriving and selecting type annotations. Our results empirically indicate that the proposed technique is practical within Reticulated Python for mitigating the performance bottleneck of gradually typed programs.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

### [57] [XR and Hybrid Data Visualization Spaces for Enhanced Data Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05509)
*Santiago Lombeyda,S. G. Djorgovski,Ciro Donalek*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: XR技术通过无缝集成2D/3D可视化，提升高维数据分析的直观性和有效性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据复杂性和信息量不断增加，需要理解数据中的复杂结构、关系和现象，同时需要理解AI工具的分析结果，这要求开发新颖有效的数据可视化工具。高维数据空间的复杂性日益增长，扩展现实(XR)自然成为帮助扩展高维理解能力的候选方案。

Method: 提出在XR空间中无缝集成2维和3维交互式可视化表示的方法，通过三个案例研究展示这些方法如何实现更高效的数据分析。

Result: 展示了在XR空间中集成2D/3D可视化表示的方法，通过三个具体案例证明了这些方法能够实现更直观、更有效的数据分析。

Conclusion: XR提供了将传统数据展示无缝集成到3维虚拟数据空间的机会，能够实现更直观、更有效的数据分析。无缝集成的2D和3D交互式可视化表示在XR空间中具有显著优势。

Abstract: The growing complexity and information content of data, together with the need to understand both the complex structures, relationships, and phenomena present in these data spaces, compounded with the emerging need to understand the results produced by AI tools used to analyze the data, requires development of novel, effective data visualization tools. Much of the growing complexity is reflected in the increasing dimensionality of data spaces, where extended reality (XR) naturally emerges as a candidate to help extend our capability for higher dimensional understanding. However, humans often understand lower dimensionality representations more effectively. Still, XR offers an opportunity for a seamless integration of simulated traditional data displays within the 3-dimensional virtual data spaces, leading to more intuitive and more effective data analytics. In this paper we present an overview of the benefits of seamlessly integrated 2-dimensional and 3-dimensional interactive visual representations embedded in XR spaces, and present three case studies that leverage these approaches for more efficient data analytics.

</details>


### [58] [Exploring Human-in-the-Loop Themes in AI Application Development: An Empirical Thematic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05510)
*Parm Suksakul,Nathan Kittichaikoonkij,Nakhin Polthai,Aung Pyae*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过多源定性方法探讨了AI应用中人类决策权威和监督机制不足的问题，提出了四个关键主题为HITL框架设计提供实证基础


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织在开发和部署AI应用时面临挑战，主要问题在于人类决策权威和监督在整个系统生命周期中缺乏明确规范。虽然HITL和HCAI原则被广泛认可，但关于角色结构、检查点和反馈机制的操作性指导仍然分散。

Method: 采用多源定性研究方法：1) 对客户支持聊天机器人的回顾性日记研究；2) 对8位来自学术界和工业界的AI专家进行半结构化访谈。通过五轮主题分析对1,435个代码词进行分析。

Result: 通过主题分析得出四个核心主题：1) AI治理与人类权威；2) 人在回路迭代优化；3) AI系统生命周期与操作约束；4) 人机团队协作与协调。这些主题为后续HITL框架设计和验证提供了实证输入。

Conclusion: 该研究通过实证方法识别了AI应用中人类监督的关键维度，为开发更系统化、可操作的HITL框架提供了基础，有助于解决组织在AI部署中面临的人类权威和监督机制不足的问题。

Abstract: Developing and deploying AI applications in organizations is challenging when human decision authority and oversight are underspecified across the system lifecycle. Although Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) and Human-Centered AI (HCAI) principles are widely acknowledged, operational guidance for structuring roles, checkpoints, and feedback mechanisms remains fragmented. We report a multi-source qualitative study: a retrospective diary study of a customer-support chatbot and semi-structured interviews with eight AI experts from academia and industry. Through five-cycle thematic analysis of 1,435 codewords, we derive four themes: AI Governance and Human Authority, Human-in-the-Loop Iterative Refinement, AI System Lifecycle and Operational Constraints, and Human-AI Team Collaboration and Coordination. These themes provide empirical inputs for subsequent HITL framework design and validation.

</details>


### [59] [Biometric-enabled Personalized Augmentative and Alternative Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05512)
*S. Yanushkevich,E. Berepiki,P. Ciunkiewicz,V. Shmerko,G. Wolbring,R. Guest*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究将生物识别技术路线图应用于个性化增强与替代沟通(AAC)系统，提出AAC生物识别注册器和生物识别可重构AAC通道的概念，并通过案例研究评估AI技术现状与实际需求之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将生物识别技术整合到个性化AAC系统中，帮助沟通障碍人群，特别是在半自动化边境控制等实际场景中满足社会和实践需求。当前AI技术（如手势识别、手语识别）的准确性与实际应用要求之间存在差距，需要系统性的技术路线图来指导发展。

Method: 提出AAC生物识别注册器和生物识别可重构AAC通道的概念框架。注册器用于采集和处理与增强沟通相关的生理和行为特征，将生物特征（如手势）链接到中间特征（如合成语音）。通过两个案例研究（手势识别和手语单词识别）评估AI技术现状，分析技术能力与实际需求之间的差距。

Result: 研究发现当前AI技术在手势识别和手语单词识别方面的准确性未能满足实际应用要求。通过提出的技术路线图方法，识别了社会需求（如帮助沟通障碍者在半自动化边境控制中沟通）与AI技术进步（如高级视频和语音处理）之间的具体差距。

Conclusion: 研究得出结论，需要进一步改进AI技术以缩小生物识别技术在AAC应用中的实际差距。提出的技术路线图为未来发展提供了具体建议，旨在使生物识别技术更好地服务于沟通障碍人群的实际需求。

Abstract: This study focuses on the roadmapping of biometric technologies onto personalized Augmentative and Alternative Communication (AAC), a branch of assistive technologies for people with communication disabilities. This technology roadmapping revolves around the proposed notions of an AAC biometric register and biometric-enabled reconfigurable AAC channels. The biometric register is referred to as a tool for acquiring and processing physiological and behavioural traits that are essential for augmentative and alternative communication. It links biometric traits, such as gestures, to intermediate traits, such as synthesized speech, for customizable communication channels. The proposed methodology is used to assess the gaps between the social and practical demands, such as assisting people with communication disabilities in the contemporary semi-automated border control, and the emerging advances in AI, such as advanced video and speech processing. We provide two case studies of the AAC that rely on hand gesture recognition and sign language word recognition, and conclude that the current accuracy of those AI technologies does not meet the practical requirements. The proposed roadmapping provides recommendations for further improvement to close these gaps.

</details>


### [60] [The People's Gaze: Co-Designing and Refining Gaze Gestures with General Users and Gaze Interaction Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05513)
*Yaxiong Lei,Xinya Gong,Shijing He,Yafei Wang,Mohamed Khamis,Juan Ye*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段方法开发直观注视手势：先通过协同设计工作坊收集非专家用户的自然手势创意，再由专家评审优化，形成用户基础、专家验证的手势集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着眼动追踪技术在移动设备中日益普及，基于注视的免手交互潜力巨大，但当前手势集多为专家设计，与用户自然眼动模式存在偏差，需要开发更直观的注视手势。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：1) 与20名非专家参与者进行4次协同设计工作坊，生成102个初始手势概念；2) 由4名注视交互专家评审并优化为32个手势的最终集合。

Result: 发现非专家用户会基于熟悉隐喻直观构建手势，并发展出"激活(注视停留)+动作(注视手势或眨眼)"的组合语法，以确保意图明确并缓解Midas Touch问题。专家则优先选择符合人体工学、与自然扫视对齐且可可靠区分的手势。

Conclusion: 最终形成的用户基础、专家验证的手势集及可操作的设计原则，为开发直观的免手界面提供了基础，有助于推动注视交互在眼动设备中的应用。

Abstract: As eye-tracking becomes increasingly common in modern mobile devices, the potential for hands-free, gaze-based interaction grows, but current gesture sets are largely expert-designed and often misaligned with how users naturally move their eyes. To address this gap, we introduce a two-phase methodology for developing intuitive gaze gestures. First, four co-design workshops with 20 non-expert participants generated 102 initial concepts. Next, four gaze interaction experts reviewed and refined these into a set of 32 gestures. We found that non-experts, after a brief introduction, intuitively anchor gestures in familiar metaphors and develop a compositional grammar; i.e., activation (dwell) + action (gaze gesture or blink), to ensure intentionality and mitigate the classic Midas Touch problem. Experts prioritized gestures that are ergonomically sound, aligned with natural saccades, and reliably distinguishable. The resulting user-grounded, expert-validated gesture set, along with actionable design principles, provides a foundation for developing intuitive, hands-free interfaces for gaze-enabled devices.

</details>


### [61] [From Toil to Thought: Designing for Strategic Exploration and Responsible AI in Systematic Literature Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05514)
*Runlong Ye,Naaz Sibia,Angela Zavaleta Bernuy,Tingting Zhu,Carolina Nobre,Viktoria Pammer-Schindler,Michael Liut*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: ARC系统通过集成多数据库、透明迭代搜索和可验证AI辅助筛选，降低系统文献综述的认知负荷，支持从管理性工作转向战略性探索


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统文献综述对科学发展至关重要，但当前工具生态系统碎片化导致高认知负荷，抑制了学术工作的迭代性和探索性。研究者面临多数据库查询迭代管理困难、文献规模庞大且发表速度快、自动化与学术自主性之间的张力等挑战

Method: 1) 对20位经验丰富的研究者进行探索性设计研究，识别关键摩擦点；2) 基于研究发现开发ARC设计探针，实现多数据库集成、透明迭代搜索和可验证AI辅助筛选；3) 与8位研究者进行对比用户研究，评估系统效果

Result: 对比用户研究表明，集成环境促进了学术工作的转变，使研究者从管理性开销转向战略性探索。系统通过外部表征支持战略性探索和透明AI推理，有助于可验证的判断，增强了从知识合成创建到长期维护的专家贡献

Conclusion: ARC系统通过解决系统文献综述中的关键摩擦点，支持研究者进行战略性探索而非管理性工作。集成环境结合透明AI辅助，能够增强专家在知识合成全过程中的贡献，为学术工作流程提供了新的设计方向

Abstract: Systematic Literature Reviews (SLRs) are fundamental to scientific progress, yet the process is hindered by a fragmented tool ecosystem that imposes a high cognitive load. This friction suppresses the iterative, exploratory nature of scholarly work. To investigate these challenges, we conducted an exploratory design study with 20 experienced researchers. This study identified key friction points: 1) the high cognitive load of managing iterative query refinement across multiple databases, 2) the overwhelming scale and pace of publication of modern literature, and 3) the tension between automation and scholarly agency.
  Informed by these findings, we developed ARC, a design probe that operationalizes solutions for multi-database integration, transparent iterative search, and verifiable AI-assisted screening. A comparative user study with 8 researchers suggests that an integrated environment facilitates a transition in scholarly work, moving researchers from managing administrative overhead to engaging in strategic exploration. By utilizing external representations to scaffold strategic exploration and transparent AI reasoning, our system supports verifiable judgment, aiming to augment expert contributions from initial creation through long-term maintenance of knowledge synthesis.

</details>


### [62] [Enhancing Tool Calling in LLMs with the International Tool Calling Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05515)
*Zuoyu Zhang,Yancheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: ITC是一个大规模多语言工具调用基准，包含3,571个真实API和17,540个任务，覆盖20个类别和40个国家，旨在解决现有基准在API真实性、可复现性和文化多样性方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具调用基准存在三个主要问题：依赖模拟或受限API、可复现性有限、缺乏文化和地理多样性。这些局限性阻碍了LLM在真实、全球化工具调用场景中的评估和改进。

Method: 构建了International Tool Calling (ITC)基准，包含3,571个真实API和17,540个工具调用任务，覆盖20个类别和40个国家。通过实验评估开源和闭源LLM的性能差异，并对模型在ITC上进行微调以验证改进效果。

Result: 实验显示开源和闭源LLM之间存在显著性能差距。在ITC上微调能带来显著改进，特别是对非英语查询，增强了跨语言泛化能力、推理一致性和对域外工具的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ITC为解决现有基准的局限性提供了有价值的评估框架，能够推动LLM在复杂、多工具和国际场景中的鲁棒性和性能提升，特别是在跨语言泛化和真实API交互方面。

Abstract: Tool calling allows large language models (LLMs) to interact with external systems like APIs, enabling applications in customer support, data analysis, and dynamic content generation. While recent benchmarks have advanced tool-use research, they suffer from key limitations, including reliance on simulated or restricted APIs, limited reproducibility, and a lack of cultural and geographic diversity. To address these gaps, we introduce International Tool Calling (ITC), a large-scale, multilingual benchmark designed for realistic, globally distributed tool-calling scenarios. ITC includes 3,571 real APIs and 17,540 tool calling tasks across 20 categories and 40 countries. Experiments reveal substantial performance gaps between open- and closed-source LLMs, while fine-tuning on ITC yields significant improvements, particularly for non-English queries, enhancing cross-lingual generalization, reasoning consistency, and robustness to out-of-domain tools. ITC provides a valuable benchmark for advancing LLM robustness and performance in complex, multi-tool, and international scenarios. Dataset: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/International-Tool-Calling-ITC-dataset-FAF4/.

</details>


### [63] [Human-Centered Ambient and Wearable Sensing for Automated Monitoring in Dementia Care: A Scoping Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05516)
*Mason Kadem,Sarah Masri,Anthea Innes,Rong Zheng*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 对2015-2025年痴呆症患者监测的可穿戴和环境传感技术进行范围综述，提出五项关键实施原则


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 映射快速发展的可穿戴和环境传感技术在痴呆症监测领域的应用现状，为未来技术设计和人本需求提供指导

Method: 采用范围综述方法，分析2015-2025年的实证传感研究，识别技术趋势和设计需求

Result: 提出五项关键实施原则：人本设计、个性化解决方案、工作流程整合、隐私保护、成本效益与伦理考量

Conclusion: 识别了痴呆症护理传感系统开发的差距、趋势和机遇，强调在增强自动化的同时支持患者自主性

Abstract: We conducted a scoping review to map the rapidly evolving landscape of wearable and ambient sensing technologies for monitoring people with dementia across home and institutional settings. We analyzed empirical sensing studies (2015-2025) to identify and inform future technical and human-centered design requirements. Five key implementation principles emerge: (1) human-centered design involving all stakeholders to augment rather than replace caregivers; (2) personalized, adaptable solutions that support autonomy across settings and severity levels instead of standardized approaches; (3) integration with existing workflows with adequate training and support; (4) proactive privacy and consent considerations, especially for ambient monitoring of residents and caregivers; and (5) cost-effective, ethical, equitable, scalable solutions with quantifiable outcomes. This paper identifies gaps, trends and opportunities for developing sensing systems that address the complex challenges, while enhancing automation and autonomy, in dementia care.

</details>


### [64] [CoEditor++: Instruction-based Visual Editing via Cognitive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05518)
*Minheng Ni,Yutao Fan,Zhengyuan Yang,Yeli Shen,Yuxiang Wei,Yaowen Zhang,Lijuan Wang,Lei Zhang,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: CoEditor++：基于认知结构的免训练图像编辑框架，通过分解"编辑什么"和"如何编辑"两个认知阶段，实现鲁棒、细粒度、可解释的指令式图像编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型多模态模型在基于指令的图像编辑中面临高级语义推理和视觉一致性的挑战，特别是在模糊或复杂指令下表现不佳。需要一种能够处理语义歧义并保持视觉一致性的方法。

Method: 提出CoEditor++框架，采用认知结构化设计：1) 将编辑过程分解为"编辑什么"和"如何编辑"两个认知阶段；2) 引入反思自选择机制；3) 完全基于开源组件构建，无需额外训练或微调。

Result: 在SmartEdit通用编辑基准和AltBear隐私合规基准上均达到最先进性能。与需要专门数据集训练的开源模型相比，保持显著更高的视觉一致性。与闭源模型相比，在保持可比指令遵循能力的同时，视觉一致性大幅超越。

Conclusion: CoEditor++的有效性源于其结构化认知设计而非特定模型组件，展示了认知中心化指令式图像编辑的潜力，为透明、跨领域适用的编辑系统提供了新方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in large multimodal models (LMMs) have enabled instruction-based image editing, allowing users to modify visual content via natural language descriptions. However, existing approaches often struggle with high-level semantic reasoning and visual consistency, particularly under ambiguous or complex instructions. To address these challenges, we propose CoEditor++, a cognitively structured, training-free framework that decomposes editing into "what to edit" and "how to edit" through two cognitive stages with a reflective self-selection mechanism, enabling robust, fine-grained, and interpretable editing. Built entirely from open-sourced components, CoEditor++ requires no additional training or fine-tuning, ensuring transparency and cross-domain applicability. We evaluate CoEditor++ on SmartEdit, a widely used benchmark for general editing, and AltBear, a privacy and compliance-oriented benchmark. Experimental results show that CoEditor++ achieves state-of-the-art performance in both general editing and responsible editing tasks compared with open-sourced models that require training on specialized editing datasets maintaining significantly higher visual consistency. When compared with closed-source models such as Nano Banana Pro or GPT-4o, CoEditor++ preserves comparable instruction following while still substantially outperforming them in visual consistency. Extensive ablation studies confirm that the effectiveness of CoEditor++ benefits from its structured cognitive design rather than any specific model component. Our findings suggest the potential toward cognitive-centric instruction-based image editing.

</details>


### [65] [A Closed-Loop CPR Training Glove with Integrated Tactile Sensing and Haptic Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05793)
*Jaeyoung Moon,Mingzhuo Ma,Qifeng Yang,Youjin Choi,Seokhyun Hwang,Samuel Burden,Kyung-Joong Kim,Yiyue Luo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一种用于CPR自主训练的闭环触觉反馈手套，集成了高分辨率触觉传感阵列和振动触觉执行器，通过实时压力测量和触觉反馈指导用户进行正确的心肺复苏操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心肺复苏（CPR）是关键的救生程序，有效的训练需要超越讲师指导课程的自主练习。当前训练系统通常依赖外部视听显示，限制了自主练习的便利性和效果。

Method: 开发闭环CPR训练手套，集成高分辨率触觉传感阵列测量手掌和手背的分布式压力，实时估计按压频率、力度和手部姿势。基于这些估计，通过振动触觉执行器提供即时触觉反馈，指导用户进行正确的CPR操作。

Result: 触觉传感器性能：宽范围灵敏度约0.85（0-600N），滞后56.04%，稳定性测试显示300次循环后漂移11.05%，全局信噪比18.90±2.41dB（600N时）。轻量级统计模型在亚毫秒推理时间内实现>92%的力度估计和手部姿势分类准确率。用户研究（N=8）表明触觉反馈相比视听提示减少了视觉干扰，但动态负载下需要简化模式以确保可靠感知。

Conclusion: 所提出的闭环系统展示了CPR自主训练手套的可行性，为未来触觉CPR自主训练系统提供了设计见解，特别是在减少视觉依赖和实现实时反馈方面具有潜力。

Abstract: Cardiopulmonary resuscitation (CPR) is a critical life-saving procedure, and effective training benefits from self-directed practice beyond instructor-led sessions. In this paper, we propose a closed-loop CPR training glove that integrates a high-resolution tactile sensing array and vibrotactile actuators for self-directed practice. The tactile sensing array measures distributed pressures across the palm and dorsum to enable real-time estimation of compression rate, force, and hand pose. Based on these estimations, the glove delivers immediate haptic feedback to guide the user for proper CPR, reducing reliance on external audio-visual displays. We quantified the tactile sensor performance by measuring wide-range sensitivity (~0.85 over 0-600 N), computing hysteresis (56.04%), testing stability (11.05% drift over 300 cycles), and estimating global signal-to-noise ratio (18.90 +/- 2.41 dB at 600 N). Our closed-loop pipeline provides continuous modeling and feedback of key performance metrics essential for high-quality CPR. Our lightweight statistical models achieves >92% accuracy for force estimation and hand pose classification within sub-millisecond inference time. Our user study (N=8) showed that haptic feedback reduced visual distraction compared to audio-visual cues, though simplified patterns were required for reliable perception under dynamic load. These results highlight the feasibility of the proposed system and offer design insights for future haptic CPR self-training system.

</details>


### [66] [Lexara: A User-Centered Toolkit for Evaluating Large Language Models for Conversational Visual Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05832)
*Srishti Palani,Vidya Setlur*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Lexara：一个用户中心的对话式视觉分析评估工具包，通过真实场景测试用例、可解释的指标和交互式工具包，解决LLM在CVA中评估的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估LLM在对话式视觉分析（CVA）中的表现面临三大挑战：需要编程专业知识、忽视现实世界复杂性、缺乏对多格式输出（可视化和文本）的可解释评估指标。

Method: 1）通过访谈22名CVA开发者和16名终端用户确定用例、评估标准和工作流程；2）开发Lexara工具包，包含：真实场景测试用例、可视化质量（数据保真度、语义对齐、功能正确性、设计清晰度）和语言质量（事实基础、分析推理、对话连贯性）的可解释指标（基于规则和LLM-as-a-Judge方法）、无需编程的交互式工具包；3）进行为期两周的日记研究，从初始22名开发者中选取6人评估工具包效果。

Result: Lexara被证明能有效指导适当的模型和提示选择，开发者反馈证实了工具包在评估LLM用于CVA时的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: Lexara通过用户中心的方法解决了CVA中LLM评估的关键挑战，为开发者提供了系统化的评估框架，有助于改进对话式视觉分析系统的设计和开发。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are transforming Conversational Visual Analytics (CVA) by enabling data analysis through natural language. However, evaluating LLMs for CVA remains a challenge: requiring programming expertise, overlooking real-world complexity, and lacking interpretable metrics for multi-format (visualizations and text) outputs. Through interviews with 22 CVA developers and 16 end-users, we identified use cases, evaluation criteria and workflows. We present Lexara, a user-centered evaluation toolkit for CVA that operationalizes these insights into: (i) test cases spanning real-world scenarios; (ii) interpretable metrics covering visualization quality (data fidelity, semantic alignment, functional correctness, design clarity) and language quality (factual grounding, analytical reasoning, conversational coherence) using rule-based and LLM-as-a-Judge methods; and (iii) an interactive toolkit enabling experimental setup and multi-format and multi-level exploration of results without programming expertise. We conducted a two-week diary study with six CVA developers, drawn from our initial cohort of 22. Their feedback demonstrated Lexara's effectiveness for guiding appropriate model and prompt selection.

</details>


### [67] [Challenges in Synchronous & Remote Collaboration Around Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05871)
*Matthew Brehmer,Maxime Cordeil,Christophe Hurter,Takayuki Itoh,Wolfgang Büschel,Mahmood Jasim,Arnaud Prouzeau,David Saffo,Lyn Bartram,Sheelagh Carpendale,Chen Zhu-Tian,Andrew Cunningham,Tim Dwyer,Samuel Huron,Masahiko Itoh,Alark Joshi,Kiyoshi Kiyokawa,Hideaki Kuzuoka,Bongshin Lee,Gabriela Molina León,Harald Reiterer,Bektur Ryskeldiev,Jonathan Schwabish,Brian A. Smith,Yasuyuki Sumi,Ryo Suzuki,Anthony Tang,Yalong Yang,Jian Zhao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文识别了可视化远程同步协作体验研究中的16个挑战，围绕五个核心协作活动，并提出四个研发活动方向的组织框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着远程工作和协作工具的普及，可视化在协作数据分析、创意构思、数据驱动决策等活动中日益重要。然而，远程同步协作中的可视化研究面临诸多挑战，特别是在扩展现实(XR)和人工智能(AI)技术快速发展的背景下，需要系统梳理研究方向和挑战。

Method: 通过汇集29位来自人机交互和可视化领域的国际专家，基于他们的专业知识和先前研究经验，识别和表征了16个关键挑战。这些挑战围绕五个以可视化和多模态通信为核心的协作活动展开，并考虑了XR和AI技术带来的新动态。

Result: 提出了一个组织框架，将16个挑战与四个研发活动序列对齐：技术选择、社会因素、AI辅助和评估。这为可视化与计算机支持的协同工作交叉领域的未来研究提供了系统化的研究方向。

Conclusion: 该研究为远程同步协作可视化体验的研究和开发提供了全面的挑战框架和组织方案，有助于指导未来在该交叉领域的研究方向，特别是在XR和AI技术快速发展的背景下。

Abstract: We characterize 16 challenges faced by those investigating and developing remote and synchronous collaborative experiences around visualization. Our work reflects the perspectives and prior research efforts of an international group of 29 experts from across human-computer interaction and visualization sub-communities. The challenges are anchored around five collaborative activities that exhibit a centrality of visualization and multimodal communication. These activities include exploratory data analysis, creative ideation, visualization-rich presentations, joint decision making grounded in data, and real-time data monitoring. The challenges also reflect the changing dynamics of these activities in the face of recent advances in extended reality (XR) and artificial intelligence (AI). As an organizing scheme for future research at the intersection of visualization and computer-supported cooperative work, we align the challenges with a sequence of four sets of research and development activities: technological choices, social factors, AI assistance, and evaluation.

</details>


### [68] [Measuring Perceptions of Fairness in AI Systems: The Effects of Infra-marginality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05889)
*Schrasing Tong,Minseok Jung,Ilaria Liccardi,Lalana Kagal*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 用户研究显示，在医疗决策场景中，人们对算法公平性的判断不仅取决于结果平等，还受群体间性能差异和数据分布背景的影响，挑战了简单的统计均等公平定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探讨在存在数据分布差异（次边际性问题）的情况下，人们如何评估机器学习模型的公平性。传统公平性定义如统计均等可能无法反映人类对公平的复杂判断，特别是在不同群体间存在性能差异时。

Method: 采用用户研究方法，招募85名参与者在假设的医疗决策场景中进行实验。研究设计了两种处理条件：1) 群体特定模型性能信息，2) 训练数据可用性信息。通过比较参与者在不同条件下的公平性判断来探究影响因素。

Result: 研究发现参与者并不将公平性等同于简单的统计均等。当群体间性能相等或信息不可用时，参与者偏好产生平等结果的模型；当性能存在差异时，特别是当差异与数据不平衡一致时，参与者认为保留这些差异的模型更公平。公平性判断不仅受结果影响，还受对差异原因信念的影响。

Conclusion: 研究结论强调公平性判断需要考虑分布背景，流行的群体公平性定义需要纳入对数据分布差异的理解。在系统设计中，必须考虑分布背景才能使算法公平性指标与人类期望在现实应用中保持一致。

Abstract: Differences in data distributions between demographic groups, known as the problem of infra-marginality, complicate how people evaluate fairness in machine learning models. We present a user study with 85 participants in a hypothetical medical decision-making scenario to examine two treatments: group-specific model performance and training data availability. Our results show that participants did not equate fairness with simple statistical parity. When group-specific performances were equal or unavailable, participants preferred models that produced equal outcomes; when performances differed, especially in ways consistent with data imbalances, they judged models that preserved those differences as more fair. These findings highlight that fairness judgments are shaped not only by outcomes, but also by beliefs about the causes of disparities. We discuss implications for popular group fairness definitions and system design, arguing that accounting for distributional context is critical to aligning algorithmic fairness metrics with human expectations in real-world applications.

</details>


### [69] [Non-urgent Messages Do Not Jump into My Headset Suddenly! Adaptive Notification Design in Mixed Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05893)
*Jingyao Zheng,Xian Wang,Sven Mayer,Lik-Hang Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 自适应MR通知系统根据紧急程度调整空间位置：非紧急通知显示为外围图标，中等紧急通知锚定到手部，非常紧急通知从外围渐进到中心视图，显著降低认知负荷


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MR通知系统将所有消息显示在固定中心位置，无论紧急程度如何，导致不必要的干扰和认知过载。需要设计能平衡注意力管理和信息可访问性的自适应通知系统

Method: 基于MR/VR通知设计和平静技术原则，开发自适应通知系统，根据紧急程度调整空间位置：非紧急通知显示为外围图标（通过头部移动访问），中等紧急通知锚定到用户手部，非常紧急通知从外围渐进到中心视图。通过18人受试者内研究评估自适应系统与默认集中式方法

Result: 自适应系统显著降低心理负荷(p=0.041)、时间负荷(p=0.008)和挫败感(p=0.004)，同时保持可比较的通知意识。逻辑回归分析显示，即使存在分类错误，用户仍偏好自适应系统，只要综合误分类率（干扰性+遗漏错误）低于可确定阈值

Conclusion: 研究首次提供经验证据表明，基于紧急程度的空间通知分布能有效解决MR核心可用性挑战，为平衡用户注意力管理和信息可访问性的沉浸式通知系统提供实用设计指南

Abstract: Mixed reality (MR) notification systems currently display all messages in fixed central locations regardless of urgency, leading to unnecessary interruptions and cognitive overload. Drawing from previous MR/Virtual Reality (VR) notification design work and calm technology principles, we developed an adaptive notification system that adjusts spatial placement based on urgency levels: non-urgent notifications appear as peripheral icons accessible via head movement, moderately urgent messages anchor to the user's hand, and very urgent notifications transition progressively from peripheral to central view. Through a within-subjects study (N=18), we evaluated our adaptive system against the default centralised approach. Results demonstrate that the adaptive system significantly reduces mental workload (p=0.041), temporal workload (p=0.008), and frustration (p=0.004) while maintaining comparable notification awareness. Logistic regression analysis reveals that users prefer the adaptive system even with classification errors, provided the combined misclassification rate (disruptiveness + omission errors) remains below a determinable threshold. Our findings establish the first empirical evidence that urgency-based spatial notification distribution effectively addresses core MR usability challenges, offering practical design guidelines for immersive notification systems that balance user attention management with information accessibility.

</details>


### [70] [Hierarchical Resource Rationality Explains Human Reading Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06006)
*Yunpeng Bai,Xiaofu Jin,Shengdong Zhao,Antti Oulasvirta*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出资源理性优化作为阅读行为的统一原则，认为眼动是为了在最小化认知和时间成本的同时最大化理解期望，并通过分层时间尺度实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论分别解释阅读中的眼动或理解过程，但两者之间的关键联系尚不清楚。需要建立一个统一原则来解释阅读行为如何协调感知、记忆和行动以实现高效理解。

Method: 提出资源理性优化作为统一原则，认为眼动选择是为了最大化期望理解同时最小化认知和时间成本。采用分层时间尺度组织：注视决策支持词汇识别；句子层面整合指导跳读和回视；文本层面理解目标塑造记忆构建和重读。通过计算实现验证。

Result: 计算实现成功复制了人类阅读中前所未有的广泛发现，从词汇效应到理解结果。表明资源理性原则能够解释阅读行为的适应性。

Conclusion: 资源理性为协调感知、记忆和行动提供了通用机制，为复杂认知技能如何适应有限资源提供了原则性解释，可推广到知识密集型人类行为。

Abstract: Reading is a pervasive and cognitively demanding activity that underpins modern human culture. It is a prime instance of a class of tasks where eye movements are coordinated for the purpose of comprehension. Existing theories explain either eye movements or comprehension during reading, but the critical link between the two remains unclear. Here, we propose resource-rational optimization as a unifying principle governing adaptive reading behavior. Eye movements are selected to maximize expected comprehension while minimizing cognitive and temporal costs, organized hierarchically across nested time scales: fixation decisions support word recognition; sentence-level integration guides skipping and regression; and text-level comprehension goals shape memory construction and rereading. A computational implementation successfully replicates an unprecedented range of findings in human reading, from lexical effects to comprehension outcomes. Together, these results suggest that resource rationality provides a general mechanism for coordinating perception, memory, and action in knowledge-intensive human behaviors, offering a principled account of how complex cognitive skills adapt to limited resources.

</details>


### [71] [Beyond Scores: Explainable Intelligent Assessment Strengthens Pre-service Teachers' Assessment Literacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06059)
*Yuang Wei,Fei Wang,Yifan Zhang,Brian Y. Lim,Bo Jiang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: XIA平台通过可视化认知诊断推理和对比/反事实解释，帮助职前教师培养评估素养，从分数判断转向证据推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估素养对个性化教育至关重要，但传统教师培养项目偏重理论知识，数字评估工具通常只提供不透明的分数或参数，限制了反思和迁移能力的发展

Method: 开发XIA（可解释智能评估平台），扩展统计支持并加入可视化认知诊断推理，包括对比和反事实解释；采用前后测对照研究设计，对21名职前教师进行定量任务、问卷调查和定性访谈

Result: 初步证据表明XIA支持反思、自我调节和评估意识，帮助减少评估错误；访谈显示从基于分数的判断转向基于证据的推理

Conclusion: 解释性支架能够连接评估理论与课堂实践，支持教师教育中评估素养的培养，为智能评估工具设计提供见解

Abstract: Assessment literacy (AL) is essential for personalized education, yet difficult to cultivate in pre-service teachers. Conventional teacher preparation programs focus on theoretical knowledge, while digital assessment tools commonly provide opaque scores or parameters. These limitations hinder reflection and transfer, leaving AL underdeveloped. We propose XIA, an eXplainable Intelligent Assessment platform that extends statistics-informed support with visualized cognitive diagnostic reasoning, including contrastive and counterfactual explanations. In a pre-post controlled study with 21 pre-service teachers, we combined quantitative tasks and questionnaires with qualitative interviews. The findings offer preliminary evidence that XIA supported reflection, self-regulation, and assessment awareness, and helped reduce assessment errors. Interviews further showed a shift from score-based judgments toward evidence-based reasoning. This work contributes insights into the design of intelligent assessment tools, showing how explanatory scaffolding can bridge assessment theory and classroom practice and support the cultivation of AL in teacher education.

</details>


### [72] [Skill-Adaptive Ghost Instructors: Enhancing Retention and Reducing Over-Reliance in VR Piano Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06253)
*Tzu-Hsin Hsieh,Cassandra Michelle Stefanie Visser,Elmar Eisemann,Ricardo Marroquim*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种技能自适应的动态透明幽灵指导系统，通过实时调整透明度来减少学习者对持续提示的依赖，提高钢琴指法学习的准确性和短期技能保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XR中的运动技能学习系统依赖持续提示，但恒定提示会导致过度依赖，削弱记忆和技能迁移。需要一种自适应方法来平衡指导与自主学习。

Method: 开发了技能自适应的动态透明幽灵指导系统，在VR钢琴训练应用中，幽灵手的透明度根据学习者表现实时调整。采用被试内设计（N=30），比较传统静态透明度模式与提出的动态自适应模式，并在无指导情况下进行即时测试和10分钟保留间隔测试。

Result: 与静态模式相比，动态模式在音高和指法准确性方面表现更好，错误增加有限，计时性能相当。这表明自适应透明度有助于学习者更有效地内化指法，减少对外部提示的依赖。

Conclusion: 自适应透明度在沉浸式学习环境中能有效提高运动技能学习的短期保留效果，为XR技能学习系统设计提供了重要启示，未来可扩展到长期保留和更复杂任务。

Abstract: Motor-skill learning systems in XR rely on persistent cues. However, constant cueing can induce overreliance and erode memorization and skill transfer. We introduce a skill-adaptive, dynamically transparent ghost instructor whose opacity adapts in real time to learner performance. In a first-person perspective, users observe a ghost hand executing piano fingering with either a static or a performance-adaptive transparency in a VR piano training application. We conducted a within-subjects study (N=30), where learners practiced with traditional Static (fixed-transparency) and our proposed Dynamic (performance-adaptive) modes and were tested without guidance immediately and after a 10-minute retention interval. Relative to Static, the Dynamic mode yielded higher pitch and fingering accuracy and limited error increases, with comparable timing. These findings suggest that adaptive transparency helps learners internalize fingerings more effectively, reducing dependency on external cues and improving short-term skill retention within immersive learning environments. We discuss design implications for motor-skill learning and outline directions for extending this approach to longer-term retention and more complex tasks.

</details>


### [73] [Exploring Socially Assistive Peer Mediation Robots for Teaching Conflict Resolution to Elementary School Students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06255)
*Kaleen Shrestha,Harish Dukkipati,Avni Hulyalkar,Kyla Penamante,Ankita Samanta,Maja Matarić*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 探索性研究：使用社交辅助机器人进行同伴调解角色扮演活动，评估小学生反应，发现多数学生反馈积极，机器人应用前景良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多美国K-12学校采用同伴调解解决冲突，但缺乏相关项目的学校需要替代方案。社交辅助机器人可能提供可访问的同伴调解实践选项，帮助学生学习冲突解决技能。

Method: 采用单次会话、被试间设计的探索性研究，12名参与者分为两组：一组使用两个机器人作为争议方，另一组仅使用平板电脑。通过角色扮演活动、前后测问卷和观察评估学生反应。

Result: 多数学生对活动有积极反馈，认为同伴调解实践提升了自我感觉，学会了帮助朋友解决冲突。参与者阅读水平差异影响角色扮演脚本的阅读和口述。机器人组中某些特质测量与学习相关测量存在显著相关性(p<0.05)，但机器人和平板组在自我认知和测验表现上无统计学差异。

Conclusion: 社交辅助机器人用于同伴调解实践具有潜力，学生反馈积极。未来研究需考虑参与者阅读水平差异和表达性差异，机器人组中发现的相关性可为未来研究设计提供参考。

Abstract: In peer mediation--an approach to conflict resolution used in many K-12 schools in the United States--students help other students to resolve conflicts. For schools without peer mediation programs, socially assistive robots (SARs) may be able to provide an accessible option to practice peer mediation. We investigate how elementary school students react to a peer mediator role-play activity through an exploratory study with SARs. We conducted a small single-session between-subjects study with 12 participants. The study had two conditions, one with two robots acting as disputants, and the other without the robots and just the tablet. We found that a majority of students had positive feedback on the activity, with many students saying the peer mediation practice helped them feel better about themselves. Some said that the activity taught them how to help friends during conflict, indicating that the use of SARs for peer mediation practice is promising. We observed that participants had varying reading levels that impacted their ability to read and dictate the turns in the role-play script, an important consideration for future study design. Additionally, we found that some participants were more expressive while reading the script and throughout the activity. Although we did not find statistical differences in pre-/post-session self-perception and quiz performance between the robot and tablet conditions, we found strong correlations (p<0.05) between certain trait-related measures and learning-related measures in the robot condition, which can inform future study design for SARs for this and related contexts.

</details>


### [74] [Structured Exploration vs. Generative Flexibility: A Field Study Comparing Bandit and LLM Architectures for Personalised Health Behaviour Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06330)
*Dominik P. Hofer,Haochen Song,Rania Islambouli,Laura Hawkins,Ananya Bhattacharjee,Meredith Franklin,Joseph Jay Williams,Jan D. Smeddinck*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究比较了五种数字健康干预中的行为改变技术传递方法，发现基于LLM的方法比模板方法更有效，但上下文赌博机优化并未带来额外帮助


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为改变技术是数字健康干预的核心，但选择和传递有效技术具有挑战性。上下文赌博机能够实现BCT选择的统计优化，而LLM能够提供灵活、上下文敏感的消息生成，需要研究这两种方法的结合效果

Method: 进行了为期4周的体育锻炼动机研究（N=54；9次研究后访谈），比较了五种日常消息传递方法：随机模板、带模板的上下文赌博机、LLM生成、混合赌博机+LLM、以及带交互历史的LLM

Result: 基于LLM的方法在感知帮助性方面显著优于模板方法，但LLM条件之间没有显著差异。意外的是，BCT选择的赌博机优化相比纯LLM方法没有带来额外的感知帮助性。无约束的LLM主要关注单一BCT，而赌博机系统强制进行跨技术的系统探索-利用

Conclusion: 定量和定性结果表明，对用户输入的上下文认知驱动了感知帮助性。研究为反思性AI健康行为改变系统提供了设计建议，解决了结构化探索与生成自主性之间的权衡

Abstract: Behaviour Change Techniques (BCTs) are central to digital health interventions, yet selecting and delivering effective techniques remains challenging. Contextual bandits enable statistically grounded optimisation of BCT selection, while Large Language Models (LLMs) offer flexible, context-sensitive message generation. We conducted a 4-week study on physical activity motivation (N=54; 9 post-study interviews) that compared five daily messaging approaches: random templates, contextual bandit with templates, LLM generation, hybrid bandit+LLM, and LLM with interaction history. LLM-based approaches were rated substantially more helpful than templates, but no significant differences emerged among LLM conditions. Unexpectedly, bandit optimisation for BCTs selection yielded no additional perceived helpfulness compared with LLM-only approaches. Unconstrained LLMs focused heavily on a single BCT, whereas bandit systems enforced systematic exploration-exploitation across techniques. Quantitative and qualitative findings suggest contextual acknowledgement of user input drove perceived helpfulness. We contribute design suggestions for reflective AI health behaviour change systems that address a trade-off between structured exploration and generative autonomy.

</details>


### [75] [Capability at a Glance: Design Guidelines for Intuitive Avatars Communicating Augmented Actions in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06556)
*Yang Lu,Tianyu Zhang,Jiamu Tang,Yanna Lin,Jiankun Yang,Longyu Zhang,Shijian Luo,Yukang Yan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过专业设计师实验，从VR能力与交互的视觉传达角度，形成了16条虚拟化身设计指南，并通过用户实验验证了指南的有效性，最后展示了在四个VR应用中的实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟现实（VR）允许用户超越人类限制使用各种能力，但如何触发这些能力并不总是显而易见的。从可供性（Affordance）的角度出发，研究者认为虚拟化身设计是解决这一问题的关键，理想情况下化身应该能够传达其能力以及如何激活这些能力。

Method: 研究首先选择了跨四个类别的八种VR能力，邀请12位专业设计师设计能够传达这些能力及其对应交互方式的虚拟化身。从设计结果中提炼出16条设计指南，包括通用建议和类别特定建议。然后通过两组各12名参与者的对照实验（一组使用指南，一组不使用）验证指南效果。最后在四个VR应用中展示了指南的实际应用。

Result: 参与者对指南的清晰度和实用性评价很高。外部评审确认，使用指南设计的虚拟化身在传达能力和交互方式方面更加直观。指南在四个VR应用中的实际应用展示了其可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的16条虚拟化身设计指南能够有效帮助设计师创建更直观传达VR能力和交互方式的虚拟化身，提高了VR用户体验的直观性和可发现性。

Abstract: Virtual Reality (VR) enables users to engage with capabilities beyond human limitations, but it is not always obvious how to trigger these capabilities. Taking the lens of Affordance, we believe avatar design is the key to solving this issue, which ideally should communicate its capabilities and how to activate them. To understand the current practice, we selected eight capabilities across four categories and invited twelve professional designers to design avatars that communicate the capabilities and their corresponding interactions. From the resulting designs, we formed 16 guidelines to provide general and category-specific recommendations. Then, we validated these guidelines by letting two groups of twelve participants design avatars with and without guidelines. Participants rated the guidelines' clarity and usefulness highly. External judges confirmed that avatars designed with the guidelines were more intuitive in conveying the capabilities and interaction methods. Finally, we demonstrated the applicability of the guidelines in avatar design for four VR applications.

</details>


### [76] [The Pen: Episodic Cognitive Assistance via an Ear-Worn Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06564)
*Yonatan Tussa,Andy Heredia*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨可穿戴AI的间歇性使用模式，提出"笔式"耳戴设备支持短期按需认知辅助，通过分层激活边界增强用户控制感、认知流畅性和社交舒适度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可穿戴AI设计为持续可用，但这与人们的工作和社交方式存在冲突，引发隐私担忧、干扰问题以及系统边界不清晰等不适感。需要探索一种间歇性使用范式，让用户在需要时主动调用短期辅助，不需要时则放下设备。

Method: 提出"The Pen"设备——一种形似笔的耳戴设备，支持间歇性、情境化的认知辅助。该设备通过语音和视觉上下文提供短期按需辅助会话，具有清晰的开始/结束边界，并采用本地处理。通过探索性研究分析分层激活边界如何影响用户的控制感、认知流畅性和社交舒适度。

Result: 研究发现分层激活边界能够有效塑造用户的控制感、认知流畅性和社交舒适度。清晰的开始/结束边界让用户更好地控制辅助会话，减少隐私担忧和社交干扰，同时保持认知任务的专注度。

Conclusion: 间歇性可穿戴AI设计通过明确的物理和数字边界，能够更好地融入用户的工作和社交生活，平衡辅助功能与用户自主性、隐私保护和社交舒适度之间的关系。

Abstract: Wearable AI is often designed as always-available, yet continuous availability can conflict with how people work and socialize, creating discomfort around privacy, disruption, and unclear system boundaries. This paper explores episodic use of wearable AI, where assistance is intentionally invoked for short periods of focused activity and set aside when no longer needed, with a form factor that reflects this paradigm of wearing and taking off a device between sessions. We present The Pen, an ear-worn device resembling a pen, for episodic, situated cognitive assistance.
