The device supports short, on-demand assistance sessions using voice and visual context, with clear start/end boundaries and local processing. We report findings from an exploratory study showing how layered activation boundaries shape users' sense of agency, cognitive flow, and social comfort.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [77] [Human-Data Interaction, Exploration, and Visualization in the AI Era: Challenges and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05542)
*Jean-Daniel Fekete,Yifan Hu,Dominik Moritz,Arnab Nandi,Senjuti Basu Roy,Eugene Wu,Nikos Bikakis,George Papastefanatos,Panos K. Chrysanthis,Guoliang Li,Lingyun Yu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文探讨AI时代下人类-数据交互系统面临的挑战，包括感知延迟错位、可扩展性限制、现有交互范式局限以及AI生成见解的不确定性，并提出需要重新定义人机角色、整合认知设计原则的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI技术的快速发展正在改变以人为中心的系统，对人类-AI交互、人类-数据交互和可视分析产生深远影响。在AI时代，数据分析涉及大规模、异构、多模态的非结构化数据以及基础模型（如LLMs和VLMs），这些因素给分析过程带来了额外的不确定性。这些转变暴露了人类-数据交互系统的持续挑战，需要超越传统的效率和可扩展性指标，重新思考解决方案。

Method: 本文采用系统性分析的方法，首先识别AI时代数据分析面临的核心挑战，包括感知延迟错位、可扩展性约束、现有交互和探索范式的局限性，以及AI生成见解的可靠性和可解释性不确定性。然后探讨这些挑战如何重塑用户与数据的交互方式，并提出需要整合认知、感知和设计原则到人类-数据交互堆栈的各个层面。

Result: 分析揭示了AI时代人类-数据交互系统面临的多重挑战：1）感知延迟错位问题；2）可扩展性限制；3）现有交互范式的不足；4）AI生成见解的可靠性和可解释性不确定性。这些挑战要求重新定义分析工作流程中人类和机器的角色，并需要将认知、感知和设计原则深度整合到交互系统的各个层面。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地研究了AI进步带来的挑战，并探讨了这些发展如何重塑用户与数据的交互方式。研究指出，构建AI时代以人为中心的交互式数据分析系统需要超越传统指标，重新定义人机协作关系，并将认知和设计原则融入交互堆栈的各个层面。论文还概述了该领域的局限性和开放研究方向，为未来研究提供了重要指导。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of AI is transforming human-centered systems, with profound implications for human-AI interaction, human-data interaction, and visual analytics. In the AI era, data analysis increasingly involves large-scale, heterogeneous, and multimodal data that is predominantly unstructured, as well as foundation models such as LLMs and VLMs, which introduce additional uncertainty into analytical processes. These shifts expose persistent challenges for human-data interactive systems, including perceptually misaligned latency, scalability constraints, limitations of existing interaction and exploration paradigms, and growing uncertainty regarding the reliability and interpretability of AI-generated insights. Responding to these challenges requires moving beyond conventional efficiency and scalability metrics, redefining the roles of humans and machines in analytical workflows, and incorporating cognitive, perceptual, and design principles into every level of the human-data interaction stack. This paper investigates the challenges introduced by recent advances in AI and examines how these developments are reshaping the ways users engage with data, while outlining limitations and open research directions for building human-centered AI systems for interactive data analysis in the AI era.

</details>


### [78] [Querying with Conflicts of Interest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05704)
*Nischal Aryal,Arash Termehchy,Marianne Winslett*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出一个在数据源与用户存在利益冲突时查询偏置数据的正式框架，包括检测偏置、提取相关信息以及查询重构的高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据源与用户之间存在利益冲突，导致数据源可能故意返回偏置结果以获取自身利益（如电商平台为增加收入而偏置产品排名），而现有无偏方案缺乏实施激励。

Method: 提出一个正式的查询框架来处理数据源故意返回偏置答案的场景，包括：1) 检测用户能否从偏置数据源提取相关信息的高效算法；2) 高效检测查询结果中偏置信息的方法；3) 重构输入查询以增加返回结果中相关信息量的算法。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的实验表明，所提算法高效且能在大规模数据上返回相关信息。

Conclusion: 为解决数据源与用户利益冲突导致的偏置问题，提出了一个完整的框架和高效算法，能够有效检测偏置、提取信息并优化查询，在真实数据上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Conflicts of interest often arise between data sources and their users regarding how the users' information needs should be interpreted by the data source. For example, an online product search might be biased towards presenting certain products higher than in its list of results to improve its revenue, which may not follow the user's desired ranking expressed in their query. The research community has proposed schemes for data systems to implement to ensure unbiased results. However, data systems and services usually have little or no incentive to implement these measures, e.g., these biases often increase their profits. In this paper, we propose a novel formal framework for querying in settings where the data source has incentives to return biased answers intentionally due to the conflict of interest between the user and the data source. We propose efficient algorithms to detect whether it is possible for users to extract relevant information from biased data sources. We propose methods to detect biased information in the results of a query efficiently. We also propose algorithms to reformulate input queries to increase the amount of relevant information in the returned results over biased data sources. Using experiments on real-world datasets, we show that our algorithms are efficient and return relevant information over large data.

</details>


### [79] [Efficient Vector Search in the Wild: One Model for Multi-K Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06159)
*Yifan Peng,Jiafei Fan,Xingda Wei,Sijie Shen,Rong Chen,Jianning Wang,Xiaojian Luo,Wenyuan Yu,Jingren Zhou,Haibo Chen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: OMEGA是一种K-泛化学习型top-K搜索方法，通过基于轨迹的特征训练K=1的基础模型，结合动态精炼过程处理不同K值查询，在保持高准确率的同时显著降低预处理成本和查询延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型top-K搜索模型针对特定K值训练，无法泛化到现实世界中的多K值查询场景：对于较大K值会出现准确率下降，对于较小K值则存在性能损失。为不同K值训练模型需要大量预处理时间，不适合实际部署。

Method: 提出OMEGA方法：1) 使用轨迹特征训练K=1的基础模型；2) 通过动态精炼过程预测较大K值；3) 利用top-K搜索的统计特性减少模型调用次数；4) 对较小K值实现最小性能损失。

Result: 在相同预处理预算下，OMEGA相比最先进学习型搜索方法平均延迟降低6-33%，同时达到相同召回目标。仅需基线方法16-30%的预处理时间，即可达到基线最优平均延迟的1.01-1.28倍。

Conclusion: OMEGA通过K=1基础模型结合动态精炼策略，实现了对多K值查询的高准确率、高性能和低预处理成本，解决了现有学习型top-K搜索方法在现实多K值查询场景中的泛化问题。

Abstract: Learned top-K search is a promising approach for serving vector queries with both high accuracy and performance. However, current models trained for a specific K value fail to generalize to real-world multi-K queries: they suffer from accuracy degradation (for larger Ks) and performance loss (for smaller Ks). Training the model to generalize on different Ks requires orders of magnitude more preprocessing time and is not suitable for serving vector queries in the wild. We present OMEGA, a K-generalizable learned top-K search method that simultaneously achieves high accuracy, high performance, and low preprocessing cost for multi-K vector queries. The key idea is that a base model properly trained on K=1 with our trajectory-based features can be used to accurately predict larger Ks with a dynamic refinement procedure and smaller Ks with minimal performance loss. To make our refinements efficient, we further leverage the statistical properties of top-K searches to reduce excessive model invocations. Extensive evaluations on multiple public and production datasets show that, under the same preprocessing budgets, OMEGA achieves 6-33% lower average latency compared to state-of-the-art learned search methods, while all systems achieve the same recall target. With only 16-30% of the preprocessing time, OMEGA attains 1.01-1.28x of the optimal average latency of these baselines.

</details>


### [80] [Tag-specific Regret Minimization Problem in Outdoor Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06405)
*Dildar Ali,Abishek Salaria,Ansh Jasrotia,Suman Banerjee*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: TRMOA问题：户外广告中标签特定后悔最小化，NP难且不可近似，提出公平感知的贪心轮询、随机贪心和局部搜索算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户外广告因投资回报率高而流行，电商公司通过影响提供商进行广告投放，需要满足影响力需求、预算约束并最小化总后悔值。

Method: 1. 将问题形式化为组合优化问题TRMOA；2. 证明其NP难且不可近似；3. 提出公平感知贪心轮询方法；4. 提出随机贪心算法；5. 提出局部搜索算法。

Result: 使用真实世界轨迹和广告牌数据集进行实验，验证了所提解决方案方法的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: TRMOA是NP难且不可近似的组合优化问题，提出的公平感知贪心轮询、随机贪心和局部搜索算法能有效减少后悔值并实现平衡分配。

Abstract: Recently, out-of-home advertising has become a popular marketing technique, due to its higher return on investment. E-commerce houses approach the influence provider to achieve effective advertising through their tags (advertising content), influence demand, and budgets. The influence provider's goal will be to make proper tag allocations, meet the required influence demand within the budget constraint, and minimize total regret. We formalize this as a combinatorial optimization problem and refer to it as \textsc{Tag-specific Regret Minimization in Outdoor Advertising (TRMOA)}. We show that TRMOA is NP-hard and inapproximable within a constant factor. The regret model we consider is non-monotone and non-submodular, and the simple greedy approach is ineffective. We introduce a fairness-aware greedy round-robin approach that reduces regret with balanced allocation across advertisers. To improve, we also introduce randomized greedy and local search algorithms. We have experimented with all the methodologies using real-world trajectory and billboard datasets to show the effectiveness and efficiency of the solution methodologies.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [81] [VDCook:DIY video data cook your MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05539)
*Chengwei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VDCook是一个自演进的视频数据操作系统，通过自然语言查询和参数配置，自动执行视频检索与可控合成，生成带完整溯源和元数据的领域专用视频数据集包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态数据集构建方式存在一次性构建、难以更新扩展的问题，研究人员和垂直领域团队需要更灵活、可配置的视频数据构建平台来降低专业视频训练数据集构建门槛。

Method: 系统基于自然语言查询和可调参数（规模、检索-合成比例、质量阈值）启动数据请求，自动进行查询优化，并行运行真实视频检索和可控合成模块，生成带完整溯源、元数据和多维标注的数据包。

Result: VDCook实现了动态演进的数据生态系统，支持基于MCP的自动数据摄取机制进行持续更新和领域扩展，提供多维元数据标注（场景分割、运动评分、OCR比率、自动字幕等），为后续数据"烹饪"和索引奠定基础。

Conclusion: 该平台通过基础设施级解决方案显著降低了构建专业视频训练数据集的门槛，支持社区贡献和治理驱动的数据扩展范式，将数据集转变为动态演进的开源生态系统。

Abstract: We introduce VDCook: a self-evolving video data operating system, a configurable video data construction platform for researchers and vertical domain teams. Users initiate data requests via natural language queries and adjustable parameters (scale, retrieval-synthesis ratio, quality threshold). The system automatically performs query optimization, concurrently running real video retrieval and controlled synthesis modules. It ultimately generates in-domain data packages with complete provenance and metadata, along with reproducible Notebooks.
  Unlike traditional static, one-time-built datasets, VDCook enables continuous updates and domain expansion through its automated data ingestion mechanism based on MCP (Model Context Protocol)\cite{mcp2024anthropic}, transforming datasets into dynamically evolving open ecosystems. The system also provides multi-dimensional metadata annotation (scene segmentation, motion scoring, OCR ratio, automatic captioning, etc.), laying the foundation for flexible subsequent data `cooking' and indexing\cite{vlogger}.
  This platform aims to significantly lower the barrier to constructing specialized video training datasets through infrastructure-level solutions, while supporting community contributions and a governance-enabled data expansion paradigm. \textbf{Project demo:} https://screenapp.io/app/v/WP0SvffgsH

</details>


### [82] [IntSeqBERT: Learning Arithmetic Structure in OEIS via Modulo-Spectrum Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05556)
*Kazuhisa Nakasho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IntSeqBERT：针对OEIS整数序列的双流Transformer编码器，通过连续对数尺度幅度嵌入和100个模数（2-101）的正弦/余弦模嵌入进行序列建模，显著提升序列预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OEIS中的整数序列值域跨度极大（从个位数到天文级阶乘和指数），传统基于标记化的模型难以处理词汇表外值，也无法利用周期性算术结构，需要专门设计能够处理整数序列算术特性的模型。

Method: 提出IntSeqBERT双流Transformer编码器：1）连续对数尺度幅度嵌入；2）100个模数（2-101）的正弦/余弦模嵌入；3）通过FiLM融合双流特征；4）三个预测头联合训练：幅度回归、符号分类和100个模数的模预测；5）基于概率中国剩余定理的求解器将预测转换为具体整数。

Result: 大型模型（9150万参数）在测试集上达到95.85%的幅度准确率和50.38%的平均模准确率（MMA），比基于标记化的Transformer基线分别提升8.9和4.5个百分点。消融实验显示模流贡献了15.2个百分点的MMA增益和6.2个百分点的幅度准确率提升。基于CRT的求解器使下一项预测的Top-1准确率从2.59%提升到19.09%，提升7.4倍。

Conclusion: IntSeqBERT通过双流架构有效捕捉整数序列的幅度和模结构，特别是模流通过中国剩余定理聚合显著提升了算术结构建模能力。模谱分析显示归一化信息增益与欧拉函数比值φ(m)/m呈强负相关（r=-0.851），证实复合模数通过CRT聚合能更高效地捕捉OEIS算术结构。

Abstract: Integer sequences in the OEIS span values from single-digit constants to astronomical factorials and exponentials, making prediction challenging for standard tokenised models that cannot handle out-of-vocabulary values or exploit periodic arithmetic structure. We present IntSeqBERT, a dual-stream Transformer encoder for masked integer-sequence modelling on OEIS. Each sequence element is encoded along two complementary axes: a continuous log-scale magnitude embedding and sin/cos modulo embeddings for 100 residues (moduli $2$--$101$), fused via FiLM. Three prediction heads (magnitude regression, sign classification, and modulo prediction for 100 moduli) are trained jointly on 274,705 OEIS sequences. At the Large scale (91.5M parameters), IntSeqBERT achieves 95.85% magnitude accuracy and 50.38% Mean Modulo Accuracy (MMA) on the test set, outperforming a standard tokenised Transformer baseline by $+8.9$ pt and $+4.5$ pt, respectively. An ablation removing the modulo stream confirms it accounts for $+15.2$ pt of the MMA gain and contributes an additional $+6.2$ pt to magnitude accuracy. A probabilistic Chinese Remainder Theorem (CRT)-based Solver converts the model's predictions into concrete integers, yielding a 7.4-fold improvement in next-term prediction over the tokenised-Transformer baseline (Top-1: 19.09% vs. 2.59%). Modulo spectrum analysis reveals a strong negative correlation between Normalised Information Gain (NIG) and Euler's totient ratio $\varphi(m)/m$ ($r = -0.851$, $p < 10^{-28}$), providing empirical evidence that composite moduli capture OEIS arithmetic structure more efficiently via CRT aggregation.

</details>


### [83] [Autocorrelation effects in a stochastic-process model for decision making via time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05559)
*Tomoki Yamagami,Mikio Hasegawa,Takatomo Mihana,Ryoichi Horisaki,Atsushi Uchida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究分析了基于时间序列的决策模型，发现自相关特性对多臂赌博机问题求解性能有重要影响：在奖励丰富的环境中负自相关最优，在奖励贫乏的环境中正自相关最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前实验表明，利用半导体激光器产生的光子混沌动力学求解多臂赌博机问题时，决策精度强烈依赖于时间序列的自相关特性。然而，这种自相关优势是否能用最小数学模型解释尚不清楚。

Method: 采用基于时间序列的决策随机过程模型，结合拔河原理求解两臂赌博机问题。模型包含阈值和双值马尔可夫信号的联合演化，通过数值模拟分析自相关特性对决策性能的影响。

Result: 数值结果显示环境依赖的结构：在奖励丰富的环境中负自相关最优，在奖励贫乏的环境中正自相关最优。具体而言，当获胜概率之和大于1时负自相关有利，小于1时正自相关有利，等于1时性能与自相关无关。

Conclusion: 该研究阐明了时间序列自相关特性对决策性能的影响机制，为改进无线通信和机器人应用中强化学习的决策方案提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Decision makers exploiting photonic chaotic dynamics obtained by semiconductor lasers provide an ultrafast approach to solving multi-armed bandit problems by using a temporal optical signal as the driving source for sequential decisions. In such systems, the sampling interval of the chaotic waveform shapes the temporal correlation of the resulting time series, and experiments have reported that decision accuracy depends strongly on this autocorrelation property. However, it remains unclear whether the benefit of autocorrelation can be explained by a minimal mathematical model. Here, we analyze a stochastic-process model of the time-series-based decision making using the tug-of-war principle for solving the two-armed bandit problem, where the threshold and a two-valued Markov signal evolve jointly. Numerical results reveal an environment-dependent structure: negative (positive) autocorrelation is optimal in reward-rich (reward-poor) environments. These findings show that negative autocorrelation of the time series is advantageous when the sum of the winning probabilities is more than $1$, whereas positive autocorrelation is useful when the sum of the winning probabilities is less than $1$. Moreover, the performance is independent of autocorrelation if the sum of the winning probabilities equals $1$, which is mathematically clarified. This study paves the way for improving the decision-making scheme for reinforcement learning applications in wireless communications and robotics.

</details>


### [84] [When AI Levels the Playing Field: Skill Homogenization, Asset Concentration, and Two Regimes of Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05565)
*Xupeng Chen,Shuchen Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 生成式AI压缩任务内技能差异，但将经济价值转向集中的互补资产，形成个体表现均等化却加剧总体不平等的悖论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究生成式AI对不平等的影响机制，解释为何能缩小个体技能差异的技术反而可能扩大总体不平等，这一看似矛盾的现象。

Method: 构建包含内生教育、雇主筛选和异质性企业的任务基础模型，通过模拟矩估计法匹配六个经验目标进行情景分析，并使用BLS OEWS数据进行职业层面回归验证。

Result: 模型识别出两种制度边界取决于AI技术结构（专有vs商品化）和劳动力市场制度；校准参数显示总体不平等符号由特定矩和弹性参数决定，而AI技术结构独立跨越边界。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是揭示机制而非给出不平等符号的定论；现有职业层面数据无法检验模型的任务层面预测，需要尚未大规模存在的职业内、任务内面板数据进行验证。

Abstract: Generative AI compresses within-task skill differences while shifting economic value toward concentrated complementary assets, creating an apparent paradox: the technology that equalizes individual performance may widen aggregate inequality. We formalize this tension in a task-based model with endogenous education, employer screening, and heterogeneous firms. The model yields two regimes whose boundary depends on AI's technology structure (proprietary vs.\ commodity) and labor market institutions (rent-sharing elasticity, asset concentration). A scenario analysis via Method of Simulated Moments, matching six empirical targets, disciplines the model's quantitative magnitudes; a sensitivity decomposition reveals that the five non-$Δ$Gini moments identify mechanism rates but not the aggregate sign, which at the calibrated parameters is pinned by $m_6$ and $ξ$, while AI's technology structure ($η_1$ vs.\ $η_0$) independently crosses the boundary. The contribution is the mechanism -- not a verdict on the sign. Occupation-level regressions using BLS OEWS data (2019--2023) illustrate why such data cannot test the model's task-level predictions. The predictions are testable with within-occupation, within-task panel data that do not yet exist at scale.

</details>


### [85] [NOBLE: Accelerating Transformers with Nonlinear Low-Rank Branches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06492)
*Ethan Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NOBLE是一种用于Transformer线性层的非线性低秩分支架构增强方法，专为从头预训练设计，相比基线模型能以更少训练步数达到相同性能，参数开销仅4%，训练时间开销7%，实现高达1.22倍的净训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法（如LoRA）主要用于微调阶段，而NOBLE旨在改进从头预训练的效率。通过为Transformer线性层添加非线性低秩分支，增强模型表达能力，同时保持较低的计算和参数开销。

Method: NOBLE为每个Transformer线性层添加永久性非线性低秩分支，分支结构为σ(xW_down)W_up，其中σ为可学习的非线性激活函数。研究发现CosNet（具有可学习频率和相位的两层余弦非线性，瓶颈空间中有线性投影）效果最佳。该方法不是适配器，而是架构的永久组成部分。

Result: 在250M和1.5B参数的LLM、BERT、VQGAN和ViT上的实验显示：达到基线评估损失所需训练步数减少32%（1.47倍步数加速），仅增加4%参数和7%单步时间开销，净训练时间加速达1.22倍。但发现Mixup/CutMix等随机增强会干扰NOBLE在ImageNet分类中的效果。

Conclusion: NOBLE是一种有效的预训练架构增强方法，通过非线性低秩分支显著提升训练效率，参数和计算开销极小。但需注意与某些正则化技术（如Mixup）可能存在互斥性，这可能是由于NOBLE更专注于目标函数的尖锐特征，而正则化鼓励平滑拟合。

Abstract: We introduce NOBLE (Nonlinear lOw-rank Branch for Linear Enhancement), an architectural augmentation that adds nonlinear low-rank branches to transformer linear layers. Unlike LoRA and other parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, NOBLE is designed for pretraining from scratch. The branch is a permanent part of the architecture as opposed to an adapter for finetuning on top of frozen weights. The branch computes σ(xWdown)Wup where σ is a learnable nonlinearity. We evaluate several activation functions and find that CosNet, a two-layer cosine nonlinearity with learnable frequency and phase with a linear projection in between them in the bottleneck space, performs best. NOBLE achieves substantial improvements with minimal overhead: up to 1.47x step speedup to reach baseline eval loss (up to 32% fewer training steps), with as low as 4% additional parameters and 7% step time overhead, resulting in up to 1.22x net wallclock speedup. Experiments on LLMs (250M and 1.5B parameters), BERT, VQGAN, and ViT consistently show improved training efficiency. We identify one caveat: Mixup/CutMix augmentation interferes with NOBLE's benefits in Imagenet classification along with other stochastic augmentations, but when disabled, ViT also improves. This discrepancy is possibly explained by regularization techniques that encourage smoother fits to the target function while NOBLE may specialize more in sharper aspects of the target function.

</details>


### [86] [Aligning the True Semantics: Constrained Decoupling and Distribution Sampling for Cross-Modal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05566)
*Xiang Ma,Lexin Fang,Litian Xu,Caiming Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CDDS算法，通过约束解耦和分布采样实现跨模态对齐，解决传统方法忽略嵌入中非语义信息的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统跨模态对齐方法追求嵌入一致性来实现语义一致性，但忽略了嵌入中的非语义信息。直观方法是将嵌入解耦为语义和模态分量，但面临两个挑战：1) 缺乏区分语义和模态信息的标准；2) 模态差距会导致语义对齐偏差或信息丢失

Method: 提出CDDS算法：1) 引入双路径UNet自适应解耦嵌入，应用多重约束确保有效分离；2) 提出分布采样方法桥接模态差距，确保对齐过程的合理性

Result: 在多个基准测试和模型骨干上的广泛实验表明CDDS的优越性，相比最先进方法提升6.6%到14.2%

Conclusion: CDDS通过约束解耦和分布采样有效解决了跨模态对齐中的语义-模态分离问题，实现了更好的语义一致性

Abstract: Cross-modal alignment is a crucial task in multimodal learning aimed at achieving semantic consistency between vision and language. This requires that image-text pairs exhibit similar semantics. Traditional algorithms pursue embedding consistency to achieve semantic consistency, ignoring the non-semantic information present in the embedding. An intuitive approach is to decouple the embeddings into semantic and modality components, aligning only the semantic component. However, this introduces two main challenges: (1) There is no established standard for distinguishing semantic and modal information. (2) The modality gap can cause semantic alignment deviation or information loss. To align the true semantics, we propose a novel cross-modal alignment algorithm via \textbf{C}onstrained \textbf{D}ecoupling and \textbf{D}istribution \textbf{S}ampling (CDDS). Specifically, (1) A dual-path UNet is introduced to adaptively decouple the embeddings, applying multiple constraints to ensure effective separation. (2) A distribution sampling method is proposed to bridge the modality gap, ensuring the rationality of the alignment process. Extensive experiments on various benchmarks and model backbones demonstrate the superiority of CDDS, outperforming state-of-the-art methods by 6.6\% to 14.2\%.

</details>


### [87] [CLAIRE: Compressed Latent Autoencoder for Industrial Representation and Evaluation -- A Deep Learning Framework for Smart Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06361)
*Mohammadhossein Ghahramani,Mengchu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLAIRE是一个用于智能制造的混合端到端学习框架，结合无监督深度表示学习和有监督分类，通过深度自编码器提取紧凑潜在特征，再使用分类器进行故障预测，并加入可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维工业环境中准确的故障检测面临重大挑战，因为传感器数据具有固有的复杂性、噪声和冗余性。传统方法难以有效处理这些高维数据特征。

Method: 提出CLAIRE混合框架：1) 使用优化的深度自编码器将原始输入转换为紧凑潜在空间，捕获内在数据结构并抑制无关或噪声特征；2) 将学习到的表示输入下游分类器进行二元故障预测；3) 加入后处理阶段，使用基于博弈论的可解释性技术分析潜在空间，识别对故障预测最有贡献的输入特征。

Result: 在高维数据集上的实验结果表明，CLAIRE显著优于直接在原始特征上训练的传统分类器。框架展示了将可解释AI与特征感知正则化相结合进行稳健故障检测的潜力。

Conclusion: CLAIRE框架的模块化和可解释性使其具有高度适应性，在医疗、金融和环境监测等其他具有复杂高维数据特征的领域也具有广阔应用前景。

Abstract: Accurate fault detection in high-dimensional industrial environments remains a major challenge due to the inherent complexity, noise, and redundancy in sensor data. This paper introduces CLAIRE, i.e., a hybrid end-to-end learning framework that integrates unsupervised deep representation learning with supervised classification for intelligent quality control in smart manufacturing systems. It employs an optimized deep autoencoder to transform raw input into a compact latent space, effectively capturing the intrinsic data structure while suppressing irrelevant or noisy features. The learned representations are then fed into a downstream classifier to perform binary fault prediction. Experimental results on a high-dimensional dataset demonstrate that CLAIRE significantly outperforms conventional classifiers trained directly on raw features. Moreover, the framework incorporates a post hoc phase, using a game-theory-based interpretability technique, to analyze the latent space and identify the most informative input features contributing to fault predictions. The proposed framework highlights the potential of integrating explainable AI with feature-aware regularization for robust fault detection. The modular and interpretable nature of the proposed framework makes it highly adaptable, offering promising applications in other domains characterized by complex, high-dimensional data, such as healthcare, finance, and environmental monitoring.

</details>


### [88] [Why Depth Matters in Parallelizable Sequence Models: A Lie Algebraic View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05573)
*Gyuryang Heo,Timothy Ngotiaoco,Kazuki Irie,Samuel J. Gershman,Bernardo Sabatini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从李代数控制视角分析序列模型的表达能力边界，建立了模型深度与李代数扩展塔的对应关系，证明了深度增加时近似误差呈指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究可扩展序列模型（如Transformer变体和结构化状态空间模型）在表达能力与序列级并行性之间的权衡，探索模型在其表达能力范围之外操作时的误差边界和缩放规律。

Method: 采用李代数控制理论视角，建立序列模型深度与李代数扩展塔的对应关系，分析恒定深度序列模型的李代数类别及其表达能力边界，并解析推导近似误差边界。

Result: 理论分析表明误差随深度增加呈指数衰减，这与这些模型的强经验性能一致。通过在符号词和连续值状态跟踪问题上的实验验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 从李代数控制视角为序列模型的表达能力提供了理论框架，揭示了深度对误差衰减的指数效应，为理解可扩展序列模型的性能提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Scalable sequence models, such as Transformer variants and structured state-space models, often trade expressivity power for sequence-level parallelism, which enables efficient training. Here we examine the bounds on error and how error scales when models operate outside of their expressivity regimes using a Lie-algebraic control perspective. Our theory formulates a correspondence between the depth of a sequence model and the tower of Lie algebra extensions. Echoing recent theoretical studies, we characterize the Lie-algebraic class of constant-depth sequence models and their corresponding expressivity bounds. Furthermore, we analytically derive an approximation error bound and show that error diminishes exponentially as the depth increases, consistent with the strong empirical performance of these models. We validate our theoretical predictions using experiments on symbolic word and continuous-valued state-tracking problems.

</details>


### [89] [A Novel Hybrid Heuristic-Reinforcement Learning Optimization Approach for a Class of Railcar Shunting Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05579)
*Ruonan Zhao,Joseph Geunes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出混合启发式-强化学习框架解决铁路车辆编组问题，针对单侧和双侧轨道访问场景，通过分解问题和引导探索提高求解效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路车辆编组是货运站场的核心规划任务，需要分解和重组车辆组以形成出站列车。单侧轨道采用LIFO堆栈结构，双侧轨道采用FIFO队列结构，组合复杂性高，需要高效解决方案。

Method: 将双侧轨道双机车问题分解为两个单侧轨道单机车子问题，提出混合启发式-强化学习框架，结合铁路专用启发式方法和Q-learning强化学习，采用状态-动作空间缩减和探索引导技术。

Result: 通过一系列数值实验证明，HHRL算法在单侧访问单机车问题和双侧访问双机车问题中均表现出高效性和高质量。

Conclusion: 混合启发式-强化学习方法能有效解决复杂铁路车辆编组问题，框架的分解策略和探索引导机制显著提升了求解性能。

Abstract: Railcar shunting is a core planning task in freight railyards, where yard planners need to disassemble and reassemble groups of railcars to form outbound trains. Classification tracks with access from one side only can be considered as stack structures, where railcars are added and removed from only one end, leading to a last-in-first-out (LIFO) retrieval order. In contrast, two-sided tracks function like queue structures, allowing railcars to be added from one end and removed from the opposite end, following a first-in-first-out (FIFO) order. We consider a problem requiring assembly of multiple outbound trains using two locomotives in a railyard with two-sided classification track access. To address this combinatorially challenging problem class, we decompose the problem into two subproblems, each with one-sided classification track access and a locomotive on each side. We present a novel Hybrid Heuristic-Reinforcement Learning (HHRL) framework that integrates railway-specific heuristic solution approaches with a reinforcement learning method, specifically Q-learning. The proposed framework leverages methods to decrease the state-action space and guide exploration during reinforcement learning. The results of a series of numerical experiments demonstrate the efficiency and quality of the HHRL algorithm in both one-sided access, single-locomotive problems and two-sided access, two-locomotive problems.

</details>


### [90] [On the Value of Tokeniser Pretraining in Physics Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05598)
*Hadi Sotoudeh,Payel Mukhopadhyay,Ruben Ohana,Michael McCabe,Neil D. Lawrence,Shirley Ho,Miles Cranmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究tokenizer预训练对物理仿真模型精度和效率的影响，发现在相同物理系统上预训练能显著提升性能，并提出了灵活时空压缩操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代高分辨率仿真产生海量多物理场数据，训练基础模型学习其动力学对复杂多物理现象建模至关重要。现有物理基础模型通常同时学习两个任务：高分辨率时空数据压缩表示提取和物理动力学捕捉，但两者同时从零学习会相互影响效果。

Method: 采用tokenizer预训练策略，先通过自编码目标预训练tokenizer，再训练动力学模型。引入灵活的时空压缩操作，将因果卷积扩展为支持运行时可调压缩比，以适应不同下游任务。

Result: 域内预训练（相同物理系统）在10,500训练步后使VRMSE降低64%，相比从零训练显著提升。域外预训练（其他系统）也有中等增益。预训练效益大小取决于领域对齐程度。

Conclusion: tokenizer预训练能显著提升物理仿真模型的效率和精度，领域对齐是关键因素。这是首次系统研究物理基础模型的tokenizer预训练，为训练高效物理仿真器提供实用指导，强调预训练数据选择的重要性。

Abstract: We investigate the impact of tokeniser pretraining on the accuracy and efficiency of physics emulation. Modern high-resolution simulations produce vast volumes of data spanning diverse physical regimes and scales. Training foundation models to learn the dynamics underlying such data enables the modelling of complex multiphysics phenomena, especially in data-limited settings. The emerging class of physics foundation models typically aims to learn two tasks jointly: (i) extracting compact representations of high-resolution spatiotemporal data, and (ii) capturing governing physical dynamics. However, learning both tasks from scratch simultaneously can impede the effectiveness of either process. We demonstrate that pretraining the tokeniser with an autoencoding objective prior to training the dynamics model enhances computational efficiency for downstream tasks. Notably, the magnitude of this benefit depends on domain alignment: pretraining on the same physical system as the downstream task yields the largest improvements, while pretraining on other systems provides moderate gains. In-domain pretraining reduces VRMSE by 64% after 10,500 training steps compared to training from scratch. To our knowledge, this is the first systematic investigation of tokeniser pretraining for physics foundation models. We further introduce flexible spatiotemporal compression operations that extend causal convolutions to support runtime-adjustable compression ratios, enabling efficient adaptation to diverse downstream tasks. Our findings provide practical guidance for training efficient physics emulators and highlight the importance of strategic pretraining data selection.

</details>


### [91] [Identifying Adversary Characteristics from an Observed Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05625)
*Soyon Choi,Scott Alfeld,Meiyi Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个识别攻击者特征的防御框架，而非直接防御攻击本身，通过分析攻击行为推断最可能的攻击者，用于系统外缓解和针对性防御


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习系统面临数据操纵攻击威胁，现有防御机制主要针对攻击本身或系统层面，但缺乏对攻击者特征的识别，限制了防御的针对性和效果

Method: 提出领域无关的攻击者识别框架，首先证明攻击者在无额外知识情况下不可识别，然后设计概率推断方法识别最可能的攻击者，并在多种学习器上实例化验证

Result: 证明了攻击者非可识别性，但提出的框架能够有效识别最可能的攻击者，该信息可用于系统外缓解措施（如限制攻击者能力）和针对性防御（如对抗正则化）

Conclusion: 通过关注攻击者而非攻击本身，提供了一种新的防御视角，攻击者识别框架能够增强防御系统的适应性和针对性，为机器学习安全提供补充防御策略

Abstract: When used in automated decision-making systems, machine learning (ML) models are vulnerable to data-manipulation attacks. Some defense mechanisms (e.g., adversarial regularization) directly affect the ML models while others (e.g., anomaly detection) act within the broader system. In this paper we consider a different task for defending the adversary, focusing on the attacker, rather than the attack. We present and demonstrate a framework for identifying characteristics about the attacker from an observed attack.
  We prove that, without additional knowledge, the attacker is non-identifiable (multiple potential attackers would perform the same observed attack). To address this challenge, we propose a domain-agnostic framework to identify the most probable attacker. This framework aids the defender in two ways. First, knowledge about the attacker can be leveraged for exogenous mitigation (i.e., addressing the vulnerability by altering the decision-making system outside the learning algorithm and/or limiting the attacker's capability). Second, when implementing defense methods that directly affect the learning process (e.g., adversarial regularization), knowledge of the specific attacker improves performance. We present the details of our framework and illustrate its applicability through specific instantiations on a variety of learners.

</details>


### [92] [The Value of Graph-based Encoding in NBA Salary Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05671)
*Junhao Su,David Grimsman,Christopher Archibald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用知识图谱嵌入来改进NBA球员薪资预测，特别是针对资深球员和高薪球员的传统机器学习方法失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于表格数据的监督学习方法在预测NBA球员薪资时存在局限性，特别是对于资深球员和薪资分布高端尾部的球员预测效果不佳，因为这些球员的薪资受到场上和场下多种复杂因素的影响。

Method: 构建包含场上和场下数据的知识图谱，将图谱嵌入到向量空间中，然后将这些向量表示整合到表格数据中，使用监督学习进行薪资预测。研究比较了多种图嵌入算法。

Result: 知识图谱嵌入方法显著改善了NBA薪资预测的准确性，特别是对于传统方法难以处理的资深球员和高薪球员。不同图嵌入算法的比较为该方法提供了实证支持。

Conclusion: 将知识图谱嵌入整合到表格数据中是改进NBA球员薪资预测的关键方法，能够帮助机器学习模型更好地理解影响薪资的复杂因素，该方法对于处理非线性和多因素影响的薪资预测问题至关重要。

Abstract: Market valuations for professional athletes is a difficult problem, given the amount of variability in performance and location from year to year. In the National Basketball Association (NBA), a straightforward way to address this problem is to build a tabular data set and use supervised machine learning to predict a player's salary based on the player's performance in the previous year. For younger players, whose contracts are mostly built on draft position, this approach works well, however it can fail for veterans or those whose salaries are on the high tail of the distribution. In this paper, we show that building a knowledge graph with on and off court data, embedding that graph in a vector space, and including that vector in the tabular data allows the supervised learning to better understand the landscape of factors that affect salary. We compare several graph embedding algorithms and show that such a process is vital to NBA salary prediction.

</details>


### [93] [Improved Scaling Laws via Weak-to-Strong Generalization in Random Feature Ridge Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05691)
*Diyuan Wu,Lehan Chen,Theodor Misiakiewicz,Marco Mondelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了机器学习中"弱到强泛化"现象，即使用弱教师模型生成的不完美标签训练强学生模型，学生模型能超越教师性能。通过随机特征岭回归分析，发现这种两阶段训练能显著改善测试误差的缩放规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解机器学习中常见的两阶段训练范式：使用学习模型标注数据，然后用这些数据训练更强大的模型。特别是探索"弱到强泛化"现象的理论基础，即强学生模型如何通过弱教师生成的不完美标签实现超越教师性能的泛化能力。

Method: 采用随机特征岭回归(RFRR)作为分析框架，推导了学生模型在教师生成标签上训练时的测试误差的确定性等价表达式。通过这个理论工具，系统分析了不同参数区域中学生模型相对于教师模型的性能改进。

Result: 研究发现学生模型的缩放规律可以显著优于教师模型，这种改进在偏置主导和方差主导的设置中都能实现。最令人惊讶的是，无论教师模型的缩放规律如何，学生模型都能达到极小极大最优速率，即使教师模型的测试误差不随样本量衰减。

Conclusion: 弱到强泛化现象具有坚实的理论基础，两阶段训练范式能实质性改善模型的缩放规律。学生模型通过弱教师生成的不完美标签训练，不仅能超越教师性能，还能达到理论最优的学习速率，这为实际机器学习中的师生学习范式提供了理论支持。

Abstract: It is increasingly common in machine learning to use learned models to label data and then employ such data to train more capable models. The phenomenon of weak-to-strong generalization exemplifies the advantage of this two-stage procedure: a strong student is trained on imperfect labels obtained from a weak teacher, and yet the strong student outperforms the weak teacher. In this paper, we show that the potential improvement is substantial, in the sense that it affects the scaling law followed by the test error. Specifically, we consider students and teachers trained via random feature ridge regression (RFRR). Our main technical contribution is to derive a deterministic equivalent for the excess test error of the student trained on labels obtained via the teacher. Via this deterministic equivalent, we then identify regimes in which the scaling law of the student improves upon that of the teacher, unveiling that the improvement can be achieved both in bias-dominated and variance-dominated settings. Strikingly, the student may attain the minimax optimal rate regardless of the scaling law of the teacher -- in fact, when the test error of the teacher does not even decay with the sample size.

</details>


### [94] [Revisiting the (Sub)Optimality of Best-of-N for Inference-Time Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05739)
*Ved Sriraman,Adam Block*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新评估了Best-of-N采样的统计最优性，发现在实际条件下（以胜率为目标而非期望奖励），适当调优的BoN在统计和计算上都是最优的，并提出了一个消除奖励攻击的变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Best-of-N采样在实践中广泛使用，但近期理论研究认为它在统计上是次优的且容易受到奖励攻击。然而，这些分析基于期望真实奖励的假设，这与实际奖励模型训练和评估方式（基于成对比较的胜率）不符。本文旨在在实际更相关的条件下重新评估BoN的最优性。

Method: 本文在更贴近实际条件的假设下分析BoN：1）以胜率（pairwise comparison-based metric）而非期望真实奖励为目标；2）考虑参考模型和奖励模型的质量；3）提出一个简单的BoN变体，通过适当调整选择策略来消除奖励攻击，同时保持统计最优性。

Result: 证明在参考模型和奖励模型质量满足最小条件时，适当调优的BoN在实现高胜率方面既是计算最优也是统计最优的，这解释了其广泛的实际成功。提出的变体能够可证明地消除奖励攻击，同时保持最优统计性能。还证明了先前方法在考虑胜率时是次优的。

Conclusion: Best-of-N采样在实际相关条件下（以胜率为目标）具有统计和计算最优性，部分解释了其实际成功。通过提出的简单变体可以消除奖励攻击问题。研究强调了在分析推理时对齐方法时选择适当目标的重要性。

Abstract: Best-of-N (BoN) sampling is a widely used inference-time alignment method for language models, whereby N candidate responses are sampled from a reference model and the one with the highest predicted reward according to a learned reward model is selected. Despite its widespread practical use, recent theoretical work has suggested that it is statistically suboptimal and vulnerable to reward hacking, the process by which models exploit weaknesses in the learned reward model to achieve high estimated reward without genuinely improving performance. We revisit this question under assumptions that more closely reflect practice than that of prior work. In particular, in contradistinction to earlier analyses that focused on expected true reward, which may not be meaningful in many practical settings, we investigate how inference-time alignment affects the win-rate, a pairwise comparison-based metric more closely aligned with how reward models are trained and evaluated in practice. We demonstrate that, under minimal conditions on the quality of the reference model and learned reward model, properly tuned BoN is both computationally and statistically optimal in achieving high win-rate, partially explaining its widespread practical success. Because BoN remains susceptible to reward-hacking in this setting, we propose a simple and practical variant that provably eliminates reward-hacking while maintaining optimal statistical performance. Finally, we show that prior approaches are provably suboptimal when considering win-rate, highlighting the importance of choosing appropriate objectives when analyzing inference-time alignment methods.

</details>


### [95] [MIRACL: A Diverse Meta-Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Objective Multi-Echelon Combinatorial Supply Chain Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05760)
*Rifny Rachman,Josh Tingey,Richard Allmendinger,Wei Pan,Pradyumn Shukla,Bahrul Ilmi Nasution*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MIRACL是一个分层元多目标强化学习框架，通过任务分解和帕累托适应策略实现少样本泛化，在组合优化问题中优于传统MORL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标强化学习（MORL）在多梯队组合供应链优化中有效，但在动态环境中部署面临任务特定重训练需求和高计算成本的限制，需要能够快速适应新任务的解决方案。

Method: 提出MIRACL框架：1）将任务分解为结构化子问题以实现高效策略适应；2）使用基于帕累托的适应策略在任务间元学习全局策略，促进元训练和微调中的多样性；3）这是首次在组合优化中集成Meta-MORL与这些机制。

Result: 在供应链领域的实证评估显示，MIRACL在简单到中等任务中优于传统MORL基线，实现了高达10%的超体积提升和5%的期望效用改善。该方法理论上与领域无关，适用于更广泛的动态多目标决策问题。

Conclusion: MIRACL展示了在多目标问题中实现鲁棒、高效适应的潜力，为动态环境中的组合优化提供了新的元学习解决方案，尽管验证主要在供应链领域，但具有更广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Multi-objective reinforcement learning (MORL) is effective for multi-echelon combinatorial supply chain optimisation, where tasks involve high dimensionality, uncertainty, and competing objectives. However, its deployment in dynamic environments is hindered by the need for task-specific retraining and substantial computational cost. We introduce MIRACL (Meta multI-objective Reinforcement leArning with Composite Learning), a hierarchical Meta-MORL framework that allows for a few-shot generalisation across diverse tasks. MIRACL decomposes each task into structured subproblems for efficient policy adaptation and meta-learns a global policy across tasks using a Pareto-based adaptation strategy to encourage diversity in meta-training and fine-tuning. To our knowledge, this is the first integration of Meta-MORL with such mechanisms in combinatorial optimisation. Although validated in the supply chain domain, MIRACL is theoretically domain-agnostic and applicable to broader dynamic multi-objective decision-making problems. Empirical evaluations show that MIRACL outperforms conventional MORL baselines in simple to moderate tasks, achieving up to 10% higher hypervolume and 5% better expected utility. These results underscore the potential of MIRACL for robust, efficient adaptation in multi-objective problems.

</details>


### [96] [Score-Guided Proximal Projection: A Unified Geometric Framework for Rectified Flow Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05761)
*Vansh Bansal,James G Scott*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Score-Guided Proximal Projection (SGPP)框架，通过近端优化平衡输入保真度和预训练分数场的真实性，解决Rectified Flow模型在语义编辑和盲图像恢复等精确任务中的控制难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rectified Flow模型虽然生成质量优秀，但在精确控制任务（如语义编辑、盲图像恢复）上存在挑战。现有方法分为两类：基于反转的引导方法存在"几何锁定"问题，严格遵循源轨迹；后验采样近似方法（如DPS）计算成本高且不稳定。需要一种统一框架来弥合确定性优化和随机采样之间的差距。

Method: 提出Score-Guided Proximal Projection (SGPP)框架，将恢复任务重新表述为近端优化问题，定义了一个平衡输入保真度和预训练分数场真实性的能量景观。该方法具有理论保证的正规收缩性质，确保分布外输入被映射到数据流形上，并有效达到流形约束下的后验模式。通过调整近端方差，SGPP支持"软引导"，在严格身份保持和生成自由度之间提供连续、无需训练的权衡。

Result: SGPP统一了最先进的编辑方法，证明RF-inversion是其框架的一个极限情况。该方法在几何上保证分布外输入被映射到数据流形，并有效达到流形约束下的后验模式。通过"软引导"机制，实现了严格身份保持和生成自由度之间的连续权衡。

Conclusion: SGPP提供了一个统一的框架，弥合了确定性优化和随机采样之间的差距，解决了Rectified Flow模型在精确控制任务中的挑战。该方法具有理论保证的正规收缩性质，能够将分布外输入映射到数据流形，并有效达到后验模式。SGPP不仅概括了现有最先进方法，还通过"软引导"机制提供了更灵活的控制能力。

Abstract: Rectified Flow (RF) models achieve state-of-the-art generation quality, yet controlling them for precise tasks -- such as semantic editing or blind image recovery -- remains a challenge. Current approaches bifurcate into inversion-based guidance, which suffers from "geometric locking" by rigidly adhering to the source trajectory, and posterior sampling approximations (e.g., DPS), which are computationally expensive and unstable. In this work, we propose Score-Guided Proximal Projection (SGPP), a unified framework that bridges the gap between deterministic optimization and stochastic sampling. We reformulate the recovery task as a proximal optimization problem, defining an energy landscape that balances fidelity to the input with realism from the pre-trained score field. We theoretically prove that this objective induces a normal contraction property, geometrically guaranteeing that out-of-distribution inputs are snapped onto the data manifold, and it effectively reaches the posterior mode constrained to the manifold. Crucially, we demonstrate that SGPP generalizes state-of-the-art editing methods: RF-inversion is effectively a limiting case of our framework. By relaxing the proximal variance, SGPP enables "soft guidance," offering a continuous, training-free trade-off between strict identity preservation and generative freedom.

</details>


### [97] [Bridging Domains through Subspace-Aware Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05768)
*Levy Chaves,Chao Zhou,Rebekka Burkholz,Eduardo Valle,Sandra Avila*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCORE方法通过解决子空间冲突来提升模型合并的领域泛化能力，在多种架构和规模上优于现有方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模型合并研究主要关注同分布或多任务场景，但在领域泛化方面的探索不足。本文旨在研究在不同领域上微调的模型合并后对未见领域的泛化能力，并解决由此产生的子空间冲突问题。

Method: 提出SCORE（Subspace COnflict-Resolving mErging）方法：1）通过计算所有模型主要奇异向量的主成分来找到共享正交基；2）将每个任务矩阵投影到共享基上；3）修剪非对角分量以移除冲突的奇异方向。

Result: SCORE在领域泛化设置中平均优于现有模型合并方法，在多种架构和模型规模上表现出有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 不同分布偏移下训练的模型合并会产生比传统多任务更强的子空间冲突，SCORE通过解决这些冲突显著提升了模型合并的领域泛化性能。

Abstract: Model merging integrates multiple task-specific models into a single consolidated one. Recent research has made progress in improving merging performance for in-distribution or multi-task scenarios, but domain generalization in model merging remains underexplored. We investigate how merging models fine-tuned on distinct domains affects generalization to unseen domains. Through an analysis of parameter competition in the task matrix using singular value decomposition, we show that merging models trained under different distribution shifts induces stronger conflicts between their subspaces compared to traditional multi-task settings. To mitigate this issue, we propose SCORE (Subspace COnflict-Resolving mErging), a method designed to alleviate such singular subspace conflicts. SCORE finds a shared orthogonal basis by computing the principal components of the concatenated leading singular vectors of all models. It then projects each task matrix into the shared basis, pruning off-diagonal components to remove conflicting singular directions. SCORE consistently outperforms, on average, existing model merging approaches in domain generalization settings across a variety of architectures and model scales, demonstrating its effectiveness and scalability.

</details>


### [98] [First-Order Softmax Weighted Switching Gradient Method for Distributed Stochastic Minimax Optimization with Stochastic Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05774)
*Zhankun Luo,Antesh Upadhyay,Sang Bin Moon,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种用于联邦学习的Softmax加权切换梯度方法，解决带随机约束的分布式随机极小极大优化问题，在完全和部分客户端参与下均获得收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统原始-对偶或基于惩罚的方法在优化最坏情况客户端性能时存在超参数敏感性和收敛振荡问题，需要一种更稳定的单循环原始切换机制。

Method: 提出Softmax加权切换梯度方法，通过软最大加权机制和切换策略处理随机约束，在联邦学习框架下实现分布式优化，支持完全和部分客户端参与。

Result: 完全参与时达到标准O(ε⁻⁴)的oracle复杂度，部分参与时通过量化客户端采样噪声获得高概率收敛保证，放松了目标函数有界性假设并建立了更紧的下界。

Conclusion: 单循环原始切换机制为优化最坏情况客户端性能提供了稳定替代方案，避免了传统方法的超参数敏感性和收敛振荡，在NP分类和公平分类任务中验证了有效性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the distributed stochastic minimax optimization problem subject to stochastic constraints. We propose a novel first-order Softmax-Weighted Switching Gradient method tailored for federated learning. Under full client participation, our algorithm achieves the standard $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-4})$ oracle complexity to satisfy a unified bound $ε$ for both the optimality gap and feasibility tolerance. We extend our theoretical analysis to the practical partial participation regime by quantifying client sampling noise through a stochastic superiority assumption. Furthermore, by relaxing standard boundedness assumptions on the objective functions, we establish a strictly tighter lower bound for the softmax hyperparameter. We provide a unified error decomposition and establish a sharp $\mathcal{O}(\log\frac{1}δ)$ high-probability convergence guarantee. Ultimately, our framework demonstrates that a single-loop primal-only switching mechanism provides a stable alternative for optimizing worst-case client performance, effectively bypassing the hyperparameter sensitivity and convergence oscillations often encountered in traditional primal-dual or penalty-based approaches. We verify the efficacy of our algorithm via experiment on the Neyman-Pearson (NP) classification and fair classification tasks.

</details>


### [99] [Sparse Crosscoders for diffing MoEs and Dense models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05805)
*Marmik Chaudhari,Nishkal Hundia,Idhant Gulati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoE与密集模型内部表征对比研究：使用跨编码器分析发现MoE学习更少但更专业的特征，而密集模型特征更广泛通用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家模型通过稀疏专家路由实现参数高效扩展，但其内部表征相比密集模型仍缺乏深入理解，需要系统比较两者的内部特征组织差异

Method: 使用跨编码器（稀疏自编码器变体）联合建模多个激活空间，训练5层密集和MoE模型（等量活跃参数）于10亿标记的代码、科学文本和英文故事数据，采用BatchTopK跨编码器并显式指定共享特征

Result: 达到约87%的分数方差解释率，发现MoE学习显著更少的独特特征；MoE特有特征比共享特征具有更高的激活密度，而密集模型特有特征则显示较低密度

Conclusion: MoE发展出更专业化、聚焦的表征，而密集模型则在更广泛、通用的特征间分布信息，揭示了两种架构在特征组织上的根本差异

Abstract: Mixture of Experts (MoE) achieve parameter-efficient scaling through sparse expert routing, yet their internal representations remain poorly understood compared to dense models. We present a systematic comparison of MoE and dense model internals using crosscoders, a variant of sparse autoencoders, that jointly models multiple activation spaces. We train 5-layer dense and MoEs (equal active parameters) on 1B tokens across code, scientific text, and english stories. Using BatchTopK crosscoders with explicitly designated shared features, we achieve $\sim 87\%$ fractional variance explained and uncover concrete differences in feature organization. The MoE learns significantly fewer unique features compared to the dense model. MoE-specific features also exhibit higher activation density than shared features, whereas dense-specific features show lower density. Our analysis reveals that MoEs develop more specialized, focused representations while dense models distribute information across broader, more general-purpose features.

</details>


### [100] [MoE Lens -- An Expert Is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05806)
*Marmik Chaudhari,Idhant Gulati,Nishkal Hundia,Pranav Karra,Shivam Raval*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoE模型专家专业化分析：DeepSeekMoE模型虽然每层激活6个专家，但主要依赖少数专业专家，单个顶级专家的输出能近似完整集成预测，为推理优化提供机会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型通过稀疏专家激活实现参数高效扩展，但其推理和内存成本优化仍具挑战性，主要原因是缺乏对专家专业化行为的深入理解。需要系统分析专家专业化模式以发现优化机会。

Method: 采用两种互补方法：1) 领域特定路由模式分析；2) 早期解码框架，追踪专家对输出表示的贡献。对DeepSeekMoE模型进行系统分析，包括令牌路由分布分析、隐藏状态相似性比较等量化验证。

Result: 研究发现：1) 尽管每层有64个可路由专家（激活6个），模型主要依赖少数专业专家；2) 顶级专家的输出与完整集成预测高度近似；3) 极少数专家处理超过50%的路由决策；4) 单专家与集成专家的隐藏状态相似度极高（部分层余弦相似度达0.95）；5) 在所有三个领域使用单个专家时，困惑度仅增加5%。

Conclusion: MoE模型表现出集中化专业知识，突显了通过针对性专家剪枝进行推理优化的潜力，同时能保持模型性能。这为研究这些模型中学习知识的定位开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Mixture of Experts (MoE) models enable parameter-efficient scaling through sparse expert activations, yet optimizing their inference and memory costs remains challenging due to limited understanding of their specialization behavior. We present a systematic analysis of expert specialization in MoEs through two complementary approaches: domain-specific routing patterns and an early decoding framework that tracks expert contributions to output representations. Our analysis of the DeepSeekMoE model reveals that despite having 64 routed experts with 6 active for each layer's computation, the model predominantly relies on a few specialized experts, with the top-weighted expert's output closely approximating the full ensemble prediction. We quantitatively validate these findings through a systematic analysis of the token routing distribution, demonstrating that very few experts handle over 50\% of routing decisions across different specialized domains. Hidden state similarity between single and ensemble experts for every layer is extremely high, with some layers having cosine similarity as high as 0.95 and perplexity increasing by only 5\% when using a single expert across all three domains. Our results indicate that Mixture of Experts models exhibit concentrated expertise highlighting potential opportunities for inference optimization through targeted expert pruning while maintaining model performance and opening avenues towards studying localization of learned knowledge in these models.

</details>


### [101] [Tiny, Hardware-Independent, Compression-based Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06359)
*Charles Meyers,Aaron MacSween,Erik Elmroth,Tommy Löfstedt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出改进归一化压缩距离(NCD)用于客户端机器学习，证明NCD并非严格度量但性能优异，开发了核方法扩展并优化训练时间，实现仅需少量用户数据即可在客户端设备上运行的高效模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线平台与用户之间的隐私冲突日益突出，客户端数据存储和分析成为趋势。但现有机器学习方法需要大量标注数据且计算成本高，不适合客户端设备。需要开发能够在客户端运行、仅使用单个用户数据、计算效率高的机器学习方法。

Method: 1) 证明归一化压缩距离(NCD)并非严格度量；2) 将NCD扩展到核方法中以处理复杂数据；3) 开发技术改进使用该距离度量的模型训练时间；4) 在分类任务中评估NCD与其他度量和核方法的性能对比。

Result: NCD性能与其他度量和核方法相当甚至更好，仅需略微增加计算成本，且尽管缺乏正式度量属性仍表现优异。最终模型仅需极少量样本即可达到显著准确度，足够轻量高效，可在客户端设备上仅使用用户提供的数据运行。

Conclusion: 归一化压缩距离为客户端机器学习提供了有效解决方案，能够在保护用户隐私的同时实现高性能分类，解决了集中式机器学习与用户隐私保护之间的矛盾，为客户端设备上的高效机器学习模型开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The recent developments in machine learning have highlighted a conflict between online platforms and their users in terms of privacy. The importance of user privacy and the struggle for power over user data has been intensified as regulators and operators attempt to police online platforms. As users have become increasingly aware of privacy issues, client-side data storage, management, and analysis have become a favoured approach to large-scale centralised machine learning. However, state-of-the-art machine learning methods require vast amounts of labelled user data, making them unsuitable for models that reside client-side and only have access to a single user's data. State-of-the-art methods are also computationally expensive, which degrades the user experience on compute-limited hardware and also reduces battery life. A recent alternative approach has proven remarkably successful in classification tasks across a wide variety of data -- using a compression-based distance measure (called normalised compression distance) to measure the distance between generic objects in classical distance-based machine learning methods. In this work, we demonstrate that the normalised compression distance is actually not a metric; develop it for the wider context of kernel methods to allow modelling of complex data; and present techniques to improve the training time of models that use this distance measure. We demonstrate that the normalised compression distance works as well as and sometimes better than other metrics and kernels -- while requiring only marginally more computational costs and in spite of the lack of formal metric properties. The end results is a simple model with remarkable accuracy even when trained on a very small number of samples allowing for models that are small and effective enough to run entirely on a client device using only user-supplied data.

</details>


### [102] [Self-Auditing Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for Few-Shot 3D Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05822)
*Son Thai Ly,Hien V. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SEA-PEFT：一种用于医学影像分割的自适应参数高效微调方法，通过在线搜索-审计-分配循环自动配置适配器，在少样本3D场景中显著提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型适应新临床站点面临挑战：领域偏移、标注稀缺，且许多临床团队缺乏AI工程师来调整适配器设计和训练方案，导致适应周期长达数周至数月。现有PEFT方法要么需要手动配置适配器，要么自动化搜索在少样本3D场景中计算不可行

Method: SEA-PEFT将适配器配置视为在线分配问题，通过搜索-审计-分配循环解决：训练活跃适配器，通过暂时关闭每个适配器来估计其Dice效用，然后使用贪婪背包分配器在参数预算下重新选择活跃集。采用指数移动平均和四分位距平滑，以及有限状态排名控制器来稳定循环并提高高噪声少样本场景下的可靠性

Result: 在TotalSegmentator和FLARE'22数据集上，SEA-PEFT在1/5/10-shot设置中比最强的固定拓扑PEFT基线平均Dice提升2.4-2.8个百分点，同时训练参数少于1%

Conclusion: SEA-PEFT通过自动化适配器配置过程，显著减少了临床站点适应的工程负担，在少样本3D医学影像分割任务中实现了更好的性能，同时保持参数效率

Abstract: Adapting foundation models to new clinical sites remains challenging in practice. Domain shift and scarce annotations must be handled by experts, yet many clinical groups do not have ready access to skilled AI engineers to tune adapter designs and training recipes. As a result, adaptation cycles can stretch from weeks to months, particularly in few-shot settings. Existing PEFT methods either require manual adapter configuration or automated searches that are computationally infeasible in few-shot 3D settings. We propose SEA-PEFT (SElf-Auditing Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning) to automate this process. SEA-PEFT treats adapter configuration as an online allocation problem solved during fine-tuning rather than through manual, fixed-topology choices. SEA-PEFT uses a search-audit-allocate loop that trains active adapters, estimates each adapter's Dice utility by momentarily toggling it off, and then reselects the active set under a parameter budget using a greedy knapsack allocator. Exponential Moving Average and Interquartile Range smoothing, together with a Finite-State Ranking controller, stabilize the loop and improve reliability in high-noise few-shot regimes. On TotalSegmentator and FLARE'22, SEA-PEFT improves mean Dice by 2.4--2.8 points over the strongest fixed-topology PEFT baselines across 1/5/10-shot settings while training <1% of parameters. For reproducibility purposes, we made our code publicly available at https://github.com/tsly123/SEA_PEFT

</details>


### [103] [Test-Time Adaptation via Many-Shot Prompting: Benefits, Limits, and Pitfalls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05829)
*Shubhangi Upasani,Chen Wu,Jay Rainton,Bo Li,Changran Hu,Qizheng Zhang,Urmish Thakker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文实证研究了大语言模型中的多示例提示（many-shot prompting）作为测试时适应方法，分析了其在不同任务和模型上的效果、局限性及影响因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多示例提示作为测试时适应方法能通过增加演示示例提升性能，但其可靠性和局限性尚未得到充分理解，特别是对于开源模型。需要系统研究这种基于输入空间的测试时更新机制的实际效果和边界。

Method: 通过实证研究，在不同任务和模型架构上分析多示例提示的性能变化，考察更新幅度、示例顺序和选择策略的影响。同时研究了动态ICL和强化ICL作为替代测试时更新策略，控制信息注入方式和模型行为约束。

Result: 研究发现：1）多示例提示在结构化任务中有效，因为演示提供高信息增益；2）对选择策略高度敏感；3）在开放生成任务中效果有限。总体上揭示了基于提示的测试时适应的实际限制。

Conclusion: 该研究系统描述了基于提示的测试时适应的实际边界，明确了输入空间更新在何种情况下有益或有害，为有效应用多示例提示提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation enables large language models (LLMs) to modify their behavior at inference without updating model parameters. A common approach is many-shot prompting, where large numbers of in-context learning (ICL) examples are injected as an input-space test-time update. Although performance can improve as more demonstrations are added, the reliability and limits of this update mechanism remain poorly understood, particularly for open-source models. We present an empirical study of many-shot prompting across tasks and model backbones, analyzing how performance varies with update magnitude, example ordering, and selection policy. We further study Dynamic and Reinforced ICL as alternative test-time update strategies that control which information is injected and how it constrains model behavior. We find that many-shot prompting is effective for structured tasks where demonstrations provide high information gain, but is highly sensitive to selection strategy and often shows limited benefits for open-ended generation tasks. Overall, we characterize the practical limits of prompt-based test-time adaptation and outline when input-space updates are beneficial versus harmful.

</details>


### [104] [Reference-guided Policy Optimization for Molecular Optimization via LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05900)
*Xuan Li,Zhanke Zhou,Zongze Li,Jiangchao Yao,Yu Rong,Lu Zhang,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RePO是一种用于分子优化的新方法，通过结合强化学习和参考指导来解决传统SFT和RLVR在指令式分子优化中的局限性，实现了更好的探索-利用平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监督微调(SFT)和基于可验证奖励的强化学习(RLVR)在推理任务中表现良好，但在指令式分子优化中效果不佳。这是因为分子优化数据通常只提供单个优化参考分子，缺乏逐步优化轨迹，导致SFT推理能力崩溃，RLVR由于模型缺乏有效探索而奖励稀疏，学习缓慢且优化受限。

Method: 提出Reference-guided Policy Optimization (RePO)方法：1) 从模型中采样候选分子及其中间推理轨迹；2) 使用可验证奖励（在相似性约束下衡量属性满足程度）以RL方式训练模型；3) 应用参考指导，将策略的中间推理轨迹作为上下文，仅以监督方式训练答案部分。RL项促进探索，指导项通过将输出锚定到参考来缓解奖励稀疏性并稳定训练。

Result: 在分子优化基准测试中，RePO始终优于SFT和RLVR基线（如GRPO），在优化指标（成功率×相似性）上取得改进，在竞争目标间实现更好平衡，并对未见指令风格具有更好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: RePO通过结合强化学习的探索能力和参考指导的稳定性，有效解决了指令式分子优化中缺乏轨迹数据的问题，实现了更好的探索-利用平衡，为分子优化任务提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) benefit substantially from supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) in reasoning tasks. However, these recipes perform poorly in instruction-based molecular optimization, where each data point typically provides only a single optimized reference molecule and no step-by-step optimization trajectory. We reveal that answer-only SFT on the reference molecules collapses reasoning, and RLVR provides sparse feedback under similarity constraints due to the model's lack of effective exploration, which slows learning and limits optimization. To encourage the exploration of new molecules while balancing the exploitation of the reference molecules, we introduce Reference-guided Policy Optimization (RePO), an optimization approach that learns from reference molecules without requiring trajectory data. At each update, RePO samples candidate molecules with their intermediate reasoning trajectories from the model and trains the model using verifiable rewards that measure property satisfaction under similarity constraints in an RL manner. Meanwhile, it applies reference guidance by keeping the policy's intermediate reasoning trajectory as context and training only the answer in a supervised manner. Together, the RL term promotes exploration, while the guidance term mitigates reward sparsity and stabilizes training by grounding outputs to references when many valid molecular edits exist. Across molecular optimization benchmarks, RePO consistently outperforms SFT and RLVR baselines (e.g., GRPO), achieving improvements on the optimization metric (Success Rate $\times$ Similarity), improving balance across competing objectives, and generalizing better to unseen instruction styles. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/tmlr-group/RePO.

</details>


### [105] [Stock Market Prediction Using Node Transformer Architecture Integrated with BERT Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05917)
*Mohammad Al Ridhawi,Mahtab Haj Ali,Hussein Al Osman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出结合节点Transformer架构与BERT情感分析的股票价格预测集成框架，通过图结构建模股票间关系，在S&P 500股票上实现0.80%的MAPE，优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预测方法难以捕捉金融市场中的复杂模式、噪声、非平稳性和行为动态，特别是在处理股票间交叉依赖关系方面存在局限。

Method: 将股票市场建模为图结构（节点为股票，边表示行业关联、价格相关性和供应链关系），结合节点Transformer处理历史市场数据并捕捉时空依赖，使用微调BERT进行社交媒体情感分析，通过注意力机制融合定量特征与情感特征。

Result: 在1982年1月至2025年3月的20只S&P 500股票上测试，集成模型的一日预测MAPE为0.80%，优于ARIMA(1.20%)和LSTM(1.00%)。情感分析使预测误差降低10%（财报季降低25%），图建模贡献额外15%改进。方向准确率达65%，高波动期MAPE保持在1.5%以下（基准模型超过2%）。统计检验证实改进显著(p<0.05)。

Conclusion: 集成图结构建模、Transformer架构和情感分析能有效提升股票价格预测精度，特别是在捕捉股票间依赖关系和市场情绪影响方面表现出色，为复杂市场环境下的金融预测提供了新方法。

Abstract: Stock market prediction presents considerable challenges for investors, financial institutions, and policymakers operating in complex market environments characterized by noise, non-stationarity, and behavioral dynamics. Traditional forecasting methods often fail to capture the intricate patterns and cross-sectional dependencies inherent in financial markets. This paper presents an integrated framework combining a node transformer architecture with BERT-based sentiment analysis for stock price forecasting. The proposed model represents the stock market as a graph structure where individual stocks form nodes and edges capture relationships including sectoral affiliations, correlated price movements, and supply chain connections. A fine-tuned BERT model extracts sentiment from social media posts and combines it with quantitative market features through attention-based fusion. The node transformer processes historical market data while capturing both temporal evolution and cross-sectional dependencies among stocks. Experiments on 20 S&P 500 stocks spanning January 1982 to March 2025 demonstrate that the integrated model achieves a mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) of 0.80% for one-day-ahead predictions, compared to 1.20% for ARIMA and 1.00% for LSTM. Sentiment analysis reduces prediction error by 10% overall and 25% during earnings announcements, while graph-based modeling contributes an additional 15% improvement by capturing inter-stock dependencies. Directional accuracy reaches 65% for one-day forecasts. Statistical validation through paired t-tests confirms these improvements (p < 0.05 for all comparisons). The model maintains MAPE below 1.5% during high-volatility periods where baseline models exceed 2%.

</details>


### [106] [Design Experiments to Compare Multi-armed Bandit Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05919)
*Huiling Meng,Ningyuan Chen,Xuefeng Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Artificial Replay（AR）实验设计，用于在线平台比较多臂老虎机算法，通过重用第一个策略的记录来减少实验成本，实现无偏估计和次线性方差增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线平台需要比较UCB、Thompson Sampling等多臂老虎机算法，但传统方法需要多次独立重启算法，成本高昂且延迟部署决策。每个算法运行产生一个依赖轨迹，可靠推断需要大量独立重启。

Method: 提出Artificial Replay（AR）实验设计：首先运行一个策略并记录其轨迹；当执行第二个策略时，如果选择第一个策略已经采取过的动作，则重用记录中的奖励，否则查询真实环境。开发新的分析框架证明该估计器的三个关键性质。

Result: AR估计器具有无偏性；仅需T+o(T)次用户交互而非2T次，当两个策略都有次线性遗憾时实验成本几乎减半；方差在T中次线性增长，而朴素设计的估计器方差线性增长。在UCB、Thompson Sampling和ε-greedy策略的数值实验中证实了这些理论优势。

Conclusion: Artificial Replay提供了一种有效的实验设计方法，显著降低了在线平台比较多臂老虎机算法的成本，同时保证了估计的可靠性和统计效率。

Abstract: Online platforms routinely compare multi-armed bandit algorithms, such as UCB and Thompson Sampling, to select the best-performing policy. Unlike standard A/B tests for static treatments, each run of a bandit algorithm over $T$ users produces only one dependent trajectory, because the algorithm's decisions depend on all past interactions. Reliable inference therefore demands many independent restarts of the algorithm, making experimentation costly and delaying deployment decisions. We propose Artificial Replay (AR) as a new experimental design for this problem. AR first runs one policy and records its trajectory. When the second policy is executed, it reuses a recorded reward whenever it selects an action the first policy already took, and queries the real environment only otherwise. We develop a new analytical framework for this design and prove three key properties of the resulting estimator: it is unbiased; it requires only $T + o(T)$ user interactions instead of $2T$ for a run of the treatment and control policies, nearly halving the experimental cost when both policies have sub-linear regret; and its variance grows sub-linearly in $T$, whereas the estimator from a naïve design has a linearly-growing variance. Numerical experiments with UCB, Thompson Sampling, and $ε$-greedy policies confirm these theoretical gains.

</details>


### [107] [Omni-Masked Gradient Descent: Memory-Efficient Optimization via Mask Traversal with Improved Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05960)
*Hui Yang,Tao Ren,Jinyang Jiang,Wan Tian,Yijie Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OMGD是一种基于掩码遍历的内存高效优化方法，在非凸设置下实现了改进的$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-3})$迭代复杂度，优于标准的${\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4})$复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大语言模型全参数训练中的GPU内存瓶颈问题。现有方法要么缺乏明确的收敛保证，要么在非凸设置下只能达到标准的${\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4})$迭代复杂度。

Method: 提出Omni-Masked Gradient Descent (OMGD)，一种基于掩码遍历的优化方法。该方法轻量级、即插即用，能够无缝集成到大多数主流优化器中。

Result: 理论分析表明OMGD在非凸设置下实现了$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-3})$的改进迭代复杂度。实证结果显示，在微调和预训练任务中，OMGD相比竞争基线均能带来一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: OMGD是一种内存高效的优化方法，具有严格改进的理论收敛保证和实际性能优势，能够有效解决大语言模型训练中的内存瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Memory-efficient optimization methods have recently gained increasing attention for scaling full-parameter training of large language models under the GPU-memory bottleneck. Existing approaches either lack clear convergence guarantees, or only achieve the standard ${\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4})$ iteration complexity in the nonconvex settings. We propose Omni-Masked Gradient Descent (OMGD), an optimization method based on mask traversal for memory efficient training, and provide a nonconvex convergence analysis that establishes a strictly improved iteration complexity of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-3})$ for finding an $ε$-approximate stationary point. Empirically, OMGD is a lightweight, plug-and-play approach that integrates seamlessly into most mainstream optimizers, yielding consistent improvements over competitive baselines in both fine-tuning and pre-training tasks.

</details>


### [108] [Agnostic learning in (almost) optimal time via Gaussian surface area](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06027)
*Lucas Pesenti,Lucas Slot,Manuel Wiedmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文改进了Klivans等人(2008)关于高斯分布下概念类L1近似所需多项式次数的分析，将所需次数从O(Γ²/ε⁴)降低到Õ(Γ²/ε²)，从而在统计查询模型中获得了(近似)最优的不可知学习复杂度界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不可知学习模型下，概念类在高斯分布下的学习复杂度与其通过低次多项式的L1近似性密切相关。Klivans等人(2008)证明了对于高斯表面积至多为Γ的概念类，需要次数d=O(Γ²/ε⁴)的多项式来实现ε近似。本文旨在改进这一分析，以获得更紧的复杂度界限。

Method: 作者改进了Klivans等人的分析，证明次数d=Õ(Γ²/ε²)就足够了。证明依赖于Feldman等人(2020)在布尔超立方体上L1近似构造的直接类比，将该方法扩展到高斯分布情况。

Result: 主要结果是显著降低了所需多项式次数：从O(Γ²/ε⁴)改进到Õ(Γ²/ε²)。结合Diakonikolas等人(2021)的下界，这为统计查询模型中多项式阈值函数的不可知学习提供了(近似)最优的复杂度界限。

Conclusion: 该工作改进了高斯分布下概念类L1近似所需多项式次数的分析，获得了更紧的复杂度界限。这一改进使得在统计查询模型中学习多项式阈值函数的复杂度达到(近似)最优，为不可知学习理论提供了重要的技术进展。

Abstract: The complexity of learning a concept class under Gaussian marginals in the difficult agnostic model is closely related to its $L_1$-approximability by low-degree polynomials. For any concept class with Gaussian surface area at most $Γ$, Klivans et al. (2008) show that degree $d = O(Γ^2 / \varepsilon^4)$ suffices to achieve an $\varepsilon$-approximation. This leads to the best-known bounds on the complexity of learning a variety of concept classes. In this note, we improve their analysis by showing that degree $d = \tilde O (Γ^2 / \varepsilon^2)$ is enough. In light of lower bounds due to Diakonikolas et al. (2021), this yields (near) optimal bounds on the complexity of agnostically learning polynomial threshold functions in the statistical query model. Our proof relies on a direct analogue of a construction of Feldman et al. (2020), who considered $L_1$-approximation on the Boolean hypercube.

</details>


### [109] [Improved high-dimensional estimation with Langevin dynamics and stochastic weight averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06028)
*Stanley Wei,Alex Damian,Jason D. Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明Langevin动力学通过平均迭代而非最终迭代，能够以n≳d^{k*/2}样本复杂度恢复隐藏方向，达到与显式平滑相同的速率，应用于张量PCA和单指标模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明梯度下降恢复隐藏方向需要n≳d^{max(1,k*-1)}样本，而显式平滑可将样本复杂度降至n≳d^{max(1,k*/2)}。本文旨在探究是否能在不显式平滑的情况下达到相同速率。

Method: 采用Langevin动力学（噪声注入随机梯度下降），但关键创新是使用平均迭代而非最终迭代。理论分析表明噪声注入与迭代平均的组合能够模拟景观平滑效果。

Result: 证明Langevin动力学通过平均迭代可在n≳d^{k*/2}样本复杂度下成功恢复隐藏方向θ*，达到与显式平滑相同的速率，应用于张量PCA和单指标模型。

Conclusion: 噪声注入与迭代平均的组合能够有效模拟景观平滑，使Langevin动力学在不显式平滑的情况下达到最优样本复杂度。推测小批量SGD也能达到相同速率。

Abstract: Significant recent work has studied the ability of gradient descent to recover a hidden planted direction $θ^\star \in S^{d-1}$ in different high-dimensional settings, including tensor PCA and single-index models. The key quantity that governs the ability of gradient descent to traverse these landscapes is the information exponent $k^\star$ (Ben Arous et al., (2021)), which corresponds to the order of the saddle at initialization in the population landscape. Ben Arous et al., (2021) showed that $n \gtrsim d^{\max(1, k^\star-1)}$ samples were necessary and sufficient for online SGD to recover $θ^\star$, and Ben Arous et al., (2020) proved a similar lower bound for Langevin dynamics. More recently, Damian et al., (2023) showed it was possible to circumvent these lower bounds by running gradient descent on a smoothed landscape, and that this algorithm succeeds with $n \gtrsim d^{\max(1, k^\star/2)}$ samples, which is optimal in the worst case. This raises the question of whether it is possible to achieve the same rate without explicit smoothing. In this paper, we show that Langevin dynamics can succeed with $n \gtrsim d^{ k^\star/2 }$ samples if one considers the average iterate, rather than the last iterate. The key idea is that the combination of noise-injection and iterate averaging is able to emulate the effect of landscape smoothing. We apply this result to both the tensor PCA and single-index model settings. Finally, we conjecture that minibatch SGD can also achieve the same rate without adding any additional noise.

</details>


### [110] [Dynamic Momentum Recalibration in Online Gradient Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06120)
*Zhipeng Yao,Rui Yu,Guisong Chang,Ying Li,Yu Zhang,Dazhou Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SGDF优化器，通过最优线性滤波原理动态调整梯度估计，解决固定动量系数导致的偏差-方差失衡问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGD及其动量变体是深度学习优化的核心，但其梯度行为的底层动力学机制理解不足。固定动量系数会固有地扭曲偏差与方差之间的平衡，导致参数更新偏斜或次优

Method: 提出SGDF（带滤波的SGD），受最优线性滤波原理启发，计算在线时变增益来动态优化梯度估计，通过最小化均方误差实现噪声抑制与信号保留之间的最优权衡

Result: 在多种架构和基准测试上的广泛实验表明，SGDF超越了传统动量方法，性能达到或超过了最先进的优化器

Conclusion: SGDF通过信号处理视角重新解释梯度更新，提供了一种动态平衡偏差与方差的有效方法，且该方法可扩展到其他优化器框架，具有广泛适用性

Abstract: Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) and its momentum variants form the backbone of deep learning optimization, yet the underlying dynamics of their gradient behavior remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we reinterpret gradient updates through the lens of signal processing and reveal that fixed momentum coefficients inherently distort the balance between bias and variance, leading to skewed or suboptimal parameter updates. To address this, we propose SGDF (SGD with Filter), an optimizer inspired by the principles of Optimal Linear Filtering. SGDF computes an online, time-varying gain to dynamically refine gradient estimation by minimizing the mean-squared error, thereby achieving an optimal trade-off between noise suppression and signal preservation. Furthermore, our approach could extend to other optimizers, showcasing its broad applicability to optimization frameworks. Extensive experiments across diverse architectures and benchmarks demonstrate SGDF surpasses conventional momentum methods and achieves performance on par with or surpassing state-of-the-art optimizers.

</details>


### [111] [DQE: A Semantic-Aware Evaluation Metric for Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06131)
*Yuewei Li,Dalin Zhang,Huan Li,Xinyi Gong,Hongjun Chu,Zhaohui Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新的时间序列异常检测评估指标，解决现有指标在点级覆盖偏向、近漏检测不敏感、误报惩罚不足和阈值选择不一致等方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列异常检测在近年来取得了显著进展，但评估实践相对滞后。现有指标存在四个主要问题：(1)偏向点级覆盖，(2)对近漏检测不敏感或不一致，(3)对误报惩罚不足，(4)由阈值或阈值区间选择导致的不一致性。这些问题可能导致不可靠或反直觉的结果，阻碍客观进展。

Method: 从检测语义角度重新审视时间序列异常检测评估，提出一种新的评估指标。首先引入基于检测语义的分区策略，将每个异常的局部时间区域分解为三个功能不同的子区域。利用这种分区，评估跨事件的整体检测行为，并为每个子区域设计更细粒度的评分机制。通过系统研究现有指标，识别与阈值区间选择相关的评估偏差，采用跨完整阈值谱聚合检测质量的方法，消除评估不一致性。

Result: 在合成和真实世界数据上进行大量实验，证明该指标提供稳定、有区分度和可解释的评估，与十种广泛使用的指标相比实现了稳健的评估。

Conclusion: 提出的新评估指标解决了现有时间序列异常检测评估指标的局限性，通过基于检测语义的分区策略和跨阈值谱的聚合方法，实现了更全面、可靠和可解释的评估，有助于推动该领域的客观进展。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection has achieved remarkable progress in recent years. However, evaluation practices have received comparatively less attention, despite their critical importance. Existing metrics exhibit several limitations: (1) bias toward point-level coverage, (2) insensitivity or inconsistency in near-miss detections, (3) inadequate penalization of false alarms, and (4) inconsistency caused by threshold or threshold-interval selection. These limitations can produce unreliable or counterintuitive results, hindering objective progress. In this work, we revisit the evaluation of time series anomaly detection from the perspective of detection semantics and propose a novel metric for more comprehensive assessment. We first introduce a partitioning strategy grounded in detection semantics, which decomposes the local temporal region of each anomaly into three functionally distinct subregions. Using this partitioning, we evaluate overall detection behavior across events and design finer-grained scoring mechanisms for each subregion, enabling more reliable and interpretable assessment. Through a systematic study of existing metrics, we identify an evaluation bias associated with threshold-interval selection and adopt an approach that aggregates detection qualities across the full threshold spectrum, thereby eliminating evaluation inconsistency. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world data demonstrate that our metric provides stable, discriminative, and interpretable evaluation, while achieving robust assessment compared with ten widely used metrics.

</details>


### [112] [Partial Policy Gradients for RL in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06138)
*Puneet Mathur,Branislav Kveton,Subhojyoti Mukherjee,Viet Dac Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种在策略梯度中建模策略结构的方法，通过优化未来奖励的子集来学习更简单可靠的策略，并比较不同策略类别的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习需要在未知环境中顺序学习行动，但现有方法缺乏对策略结构的显式建模。需要一种能够平衡策略复杂性和学习可靠性的方法，以在不同问题中选择合适的策略类别。

Method: 提出在策略梯度中建模策略结构的方法，核心思想是优化未来奖励的子集而非全部奖励。较小的奖励子集对应更简单的策略，其经验梯度估计更准确。该方法能够建模和比较多种策略类别，包括完全规划、贪婪、K步前瞻和分段策略。

Result: 在多个角色对齐对话问题上进行实证评估，发现不同策略在不同问题上表现优异，反映了它们的不同特性，凸显了所研究策略类别的重要性。

Conclusion: 通过优化未来奖励子集来建模策略结构的方法有效，能够学习更可靠的策略。不同策略类别在不同问题中各有优势，强调了根据问题特性选择合适策略类别的重要性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is a framework for learning to act sequentially in an unknown environment. We propose a natural approach for modeling policy structure in policy gradients. The key idea is to optimize for a subset of future rewards: smaller subsets represent simpler policies, which can be learned more reliably because their empirical gradient estimates are more accurate. Our approach allows for modeling and comparison of different policy classes, including full planning, greedy, K-step lookahead, and segment policies. We evaluate the policies empirically on multiple persona-alignment conversational problems. Different policies excel in different problems, reflecting their different characteristics and highlighting the importance of our studied policy class.

</details>


### [113] [Topological descriptors of foot clearance gait dynamics improve differential diagnosis of Parkinsonism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06212)
*Jhonathan Barrios,Wolfram Erlhagen,Miguel F. Gago,Estela Bicho,Flora Ferreira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用拓扑数据分析（TDA）分析足部间隙时间序列，结合随机森林分类器，可有效区分特发性帕金森病与血管性帕金森病，准确率达83%，AUC=0.89。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森综合征的鉴别诊断具有挑战性，因为运动症状和步态异常存在重叠。传统步态分析方法常忽略足部间隙模式中隐藏的非线性和结构特征，需要更精确的鉴别工具来指导治疗和预后。

Method: 采用拓扑数据分析（TDA）处理足部间隙时间序列，通过持续同调生成Betti曲线、持续景观和轮廓图作为特征，使用随机森林分类器进行帕金森综合征分类。数据集包括15名对照、15名特发性帕金森病和14名血管性帕金森病患者，采用留一法交叉验证评估模型性能。

Result: Betti曲线描述符表现最佳。在用药状态下，特发性帕金森病与血管性帕金森病的鉴别准确率达83%，AUC=0.89。用药状态下的性能优于未用药状态，且同时考虑两种状态时性能进一步提升，表明拓扑特征对左旋多巴相关的步态变化敏感。

Conclusion: 拓扑数据分析与机器学习结合可显著改善临床步态分析，有助于帕金森综合征的鉴别诊断，特别是在区分特发性帕金森病和血管性帕金森病方面表现出良好潜力。

Abstract: Differential diagnosis among parkinsonian syndromes remains a clinical challenge due to overlapping motor symptoms and subtle gait abnormalities. Accurate differentiation is crucial for treatment planning and prognosis. While gait analysis is a well established approach for assessing motor impairments, conventional methods often overlook hidden nonlinear and structural features embedded in foot clearance patterns. We evaluated Topological Data Analysis (TDA) as a complementary tool for Parkinsonism classification using foot clearance time series. Persistent homology produced Betti curves, persistence landscapes, and silhouettes, which were used as features for a Random Forest classifier. The dataset comprised 15 controls (CO), 15 idiopathic Parkinson's disease (IPD), and 14 vascular Parkinsonism (VaP). Models were assessed with leave-one-out cross-validation (LOOCV). Betti-curve descriptors consistently yielded the strongest results. For IPD vs VaP, foot clearance variables minimum toe clearance, maximum toe late swing, and maximum heel clearance achieved 83% accuracy and AUC=0.89 under LOOCV in the medicated (On) state. Performance improved in the On state and further when both Off and On states were considered, indicating sensitivity of the topological features to levodopa related gait changes. These findings support integrating TDA with machine learning to improve clinical gait analysis and aid differential diagnosis across parkinsonian disorders.

</details>


### [114] [FedSCS-XGB -- Federated Server-centric surrogate XGBoost for continual health monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06224)
*Felix Walger,Mehdi Ejtehadi,Anke Schmeink,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于XGBoost的分布式机器学习协议，用于可穿戴传感器的人类活动识别，在保持XGBoost结构优势的同时实现接近集中式性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脊髓损伤患者需要连续监测以预防压疮和血压不稳定等风险，可穿戴传感器结合本地数据处理能实现早期检测和干预。分布式机器学习能应对远程监测中的数据异质性和碎片化问题

Method: 提出受Party-Adaptive XGBoost启发的分布式协议，明确保留标准XGBoost的关键结构和优化特性，包括基于直方图的分裂构建和树集成动态。协议在适当数据条件和超参数选择下能收敛到与集中式XGBoost训练等效的解决方案

Result: 在代表性可穿戴传感器HAR数据集上实证评估，与集中式XGBoost和IBM PAX对比。结果显示该方法能匹配集中式性能，差距在1%以内，同时在分布式可穿戴HAR设置中保留XGBoost的结构优势

Conclusion: 提出的分布式XGBoost协议在理论分析和实证评估中都表现出色，能有效应用于可穿戴传感器的人类活动识别，为脊髓损伤等医疗场景的连续监测提供技术支撑

Abstract: Wearable sensors with local data processing can detect health threats early, enhance documentation, and support personalized therapy. In the context of spinal cord injury (SCI), which involves risks such as pressure injuries and blood pressure instability, continuous monitoring can help mitigate these by enabling early deDtection and intervention. In this work, we present a novel distributed machine learning (DML) protocol for human activity recognition (HAR) from wearable sensor data based on gradient-boosted decision trees (XGBoost).
